### [1] [MLFCIL: A Multi-Level Forgetting Mitigation Framework for Federated Class-Incremental Learning in LEO Satellites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02356)
*Heng Zhang,Xiaohong Deng,Sijing Duan,Wu Ouyang,KM Mahfujul,Yiqin Deng,Zhigang Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: MLFCIL是一个针对低地球轨道卫星星座的多层次遗忘缓解联邦类增量学习框架，通过分解遗忘源、多层次缓解策略和双粒度协调机制，解决数据异构性、灾难性遗忘和资源受限下的稳定性-可塑性平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低地球轨道卫星星座在严格内存和通信约束下进行协同训练面临三大挑战：轨道动态导致的非独立同分布数据异构性、聚合过程中放大的灾难性遗忘、以及有限资源下稳定性与可塑性的平衡需求。现有联邦类增量学习方法难以有效应对这些LEO特定挑战。

Method: 提出MLFCIL多层次遗忘缓解框架：1）类重加权损失减少局部偏差；2）特征回放和原型引导漂移补偿的知识蒸馏保留跨任务知识；3）类感知聚合缓解联邦过程中的遗忘。此外设计双粒度协调策略：轮级自适应损失平衡和步级梯度投影，增强稳定性-可塑性权衡。

Result: 在NWPU-RESISC45数据集上的实验表明，MLFCIL在准确率和遗忘缓解方面显著优于基线方法，同时引入的资源开销最小。

Conclusion: MLFCIL通过分解灾难性遗忘的三个来源并在不同层次进行针对性缓解，有效解决了LEO卫星星座联邦类增量学习中的关键挑战，实现了更好的性能与资源效率平衡。

Abstract: Low-Earth-orbit (LEO) satellite constellations are increasingly performing on-board computing. However, the continuous emergence of new classes under strict memory and communication constraints poses major challenges for collaborative training. Federated class-incremental learning (FCIL) enables distributed incremental learning without sharing raw data, but faces three LEO-specific challenges: non-independent and identically distributed data heterogeneity caused by orbital dynamics, amplified catastrophic forgetting during aggregation, and the need to balance stability and plasticity under limited resources. To tackle these challenges, we propose MLFCIL, a multi-level forgetting mitigation framework that decomposes catastrophic forgetting into three sources and addresses them at different levels: class-reweighted loss to reduce local bias, knowledge distillation with feature replay and prototype-guided drift compensation to preserve cross-task knowledge, and class-aware aggregation to mitigate forgetting during federation. In addition, we design a dual-granularity coordination strategy that combines round-level adaptive loss balancing with step-level gradient projection to further enhance the stability-plasticity trade-off. Experiments on the NWPU-RESISC45 dataset show that MLFCIL significantly outperforms baselines in both accuracy and forgetting mitigation, while introducing minimal resource overhead.

</details>


### [2] [TRACE: Traceroute-based Internet Route change Analysis with Ensemble Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02361)
*Raul Suzuki,Rodrigo Moreira,Pedro Henrique A. Damaso de Melo,Larissa F. Rodrigues Moreira,Flávio de Oliveira Silva*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: TRACE是一个仅使用traceroute延迟数据检测互联网路由不稳定的机器学习管道，通过特征工程和堆叠集成学习实现高精度检测，显著优于传统基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测互联网路由不稳定是一项关键但具有挑战性的任务，特别是当仅依赖端点主动测量时。传统方法需要控制平面信息，而TRACE旨在仅使用traceroute延迟数据来识别路由变化，确保独立于控制平面信息。

Method: 提出一个稳健的特征工程策略，使用滚动统计和聚合上下文模式捕捉时间动态。架构采用梯度提升决策树的堆叠集成，通过超参数优化的元学习器进行精炼。通过严格校准决策阈值来解决罕见路由事件的固有类别不平衡问题。

Result: TRACE实现了优越的F1分数性能，显著优于传统基线模型，并在检测互联网路由变化方面表现出强大的有效性。

Conclusion: TRACE证明了仅使用traceroute延迟数据检测互联网路由不稳定的可行性，通过创新的特征工程和机器学习架构，为路由监控提供了独立于控制平面的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Detecting Internet routing instability is a critical yet challenging task, particularly when relying solely on endpoint active measurements. This study introduces TRACE, a MachineLearning (ML)pipeline designed to identify route changes using only traceroute latency data, thereby ensuring independence from control plane information. We propose a robust feature engineering strategy that captures temporal dynamics using rolling statistics and aggregated context patterns. The architecture leverages a stacked ensemble of Gradient Boosted Decision Trees refined by a hyperparameter-optimized meta-learner. By strictly calibrating decision thresholds to address the inherent class imbalance of rare routing events, TRACE achieves a superior F1-score performance, significantly outperforming traditional baseline models and demonstrating strong effective ness in detecting routing changes on the Internet.

</details>


### [3] [Evaluating Small Language Models for Front-Door Routing: A Harmonized Benchmark and Synthetic-Traffic Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02367)
*Warren Johnson,Charles Lee*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 小型语言模型（SLMs，1-4B参数）已具备足够的推理能力，可用于零边际成本、自托管的实时任务分类，从而优化多模型路由决策，但准确率仍需提升6-8个百分点才能达到生产可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路由方法依赖大型语言模型分类器或偏好训练的路由器，这些方法成本高、延迟大，且将多目标优化简化为单维质量预测。研究旨在探索小型语言模型是否已具备足够推理能力，以低成本、低延迟方式实现任务分类，从而优化模型路由决策。

Method: 通过两个研究验证：研究1是离线基准测试，在相同硬件、服务栈和量化条件下，比较Phi-3.5-mini、Qwen2.5-1.5B和Qwen-2.5-3B在六标签分类任务上的性能；研究2是预注册的四臂随机实验，在合成流量下比较Phi-4-mini、Qwen-2.5-3B、DeepSeek-V3和无路由控制组，评估准确率、延迟和成本。

Result: 研究1中Qwen-2.5-3B获得最佳精确匹配准确率（0.783），具有最强的延迟-准确率权衡，且是唯一在所有六个任务家族上都有非零准确率的模型。研究2中DeepSeek-V3准确率最高（0.830）但未通过预注册的P95延迟门限（2,295 ms）；Qwen-2.5-3B在自托管模型中具有帕累托优势（0.793准确率，988 ms中位数延迟，$0边际成本）。但所有模型均未达到独立可用性标准（准确率≥0.85，P95延迟≤2,000 ms）。

Conclusion: 小型语言模型已满足基于路由的成本和延迟前提条件，但准确率仍需提升6-8个百分点。此外，正确分类是否能转化为下游输出质量的问题尚未测试，这些因素限制了其生产可用性。研究为低成本、低延迟的多模型路由系统提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Selecting the appropriate model at inference time -- the routing problem -- requires jointly optimizing output quality, cost, latency, and governance constraints. Existing approaches delegate this decision to LLM-based classifiers or preference-trained routers that are themselves costly and high-latency, reducing a multi-objective optimization to single-dimensional quality prediction. We argue that small language models (SLMs, 1-4B parameters) have now achieved sufficient reasoning capability for sub-second, zero-marginal-cost, self-hosted task classification, potentially making the routing decision negligible in the inference budget. We test this thesis on a six-label taxonomy through two studies. Study 1 is a harmonized offline benchmark of Phi-3.5-mini, Qwen2.5-1.5B, and Qwen-2.5-3B on identical Azure T4 hardware, serving stack, quantization, and a fixed 60-case corpus. Qwen-2.5-3B achieves the best exact-match accuracy (0.783), the strongest latency-accuracy tradeoff, and the only nonzero accuracy on all six task families. Study 2 is a pre-registered four-arm randomized experiment under synthetic traffic with an effective sample size of 60 unique cases per arm, comparing Phi-4-mini, Qwen-2.5-3B, and DeepSeek-V3 against a no-routing control. DeepSeek-V3 attains the highest accuracy (0.830) but fails the pre-registered P95 latency gate (2,295 ms); Qwen-2.5-3B is Pareto-dominant among self-hosted models (0.793 accuracy, 988 ms median, $0 marginal cost). No model meets the standalone viability criterion (>=0.85 accuracy, <=2,000 ms P95). The cost and latency prerequisites for SLM-based routing are met; the accuracy gap of 6-8 percentage points and the untested question of whether correct classification translates to downstream output quality bound the remaining distance to production viability.

</details>


### [4] [Beyond Message Passing: Toward Semantically Aligned Agent Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02369)
*Dun Yuan,Fuyuan Lyu,Ye Yuan,Weixu Zhang,Bowei He,Jiayi Geng,Linfeng Du,Zipeng Sun,Yankai Chen,Changjiang Han,Jikun Kang,Alex Chen,Haolun Wu,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个三层框架（通信、句法、语义）来分析18种智能体通信协议，发现当前协议设计存在不平衡：传输和生命周期管理成熟，但语义协调机制有限，导致技术债务和维护成本增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型系统需要工具使用、多智能体协调和异构环境操作，智能体通信协议成为关键基础设施。当前缺乏系统化的分析框架来评估这些协议如何支持可靠通信和语义协调，导致协议选择困难和技术债务积累。

Method: 提出人类启发的三层框架（通信层、句法层、语义层），系统分析18种代表性协议，评估它们在可靠传输、结构化交互和语义级协调方面的支持能力，识别技术债务形式并提供部署指导。

Result: 分析显示当前协议设计存在明显不平衡：大多数协议在传输、流式处理、模式定义和生命周期管理方面提供成熟支持，但在澄清、上下文对齐和验证等语义级机制方面有限。语义责任被推给提示词、包装器或应用特定编排逻辑，导致隐藏的互操作性和维护成本。

Conclusion: 需要超越消息传递，构建具有共享理解的互操作、安全且语义鲁棒的智能体生态系统。论文提出了具体的研究议程，包括开发协议级语义协调机制、减少技术债务，并为不同部署场景提供实用的协议选择指导。

Abstract: Agent communication protocols are becoming critical infrastructure for large language model (LLM) systems that must use tools, coordinate with other agents, and operate across heterogeneous environments. This work presents a human-inspired perspective on this emerging landscape by organizing agent communication into three layers: communication, syntactic, and semantic. Under this framework, we systematically analyze 18 representative protocols and compare how they support reliable transport, structured interaction, and meaning-level coordination. Our analysis shows a clear imbalance in current protocol design. Most protocols provide increasingly mature support for transport, streaming, schema definition, and lifecycle management, but offer limited protocol-level mechanisms for clarification, context alignment, and verification. As a result, semantic responsibilities are often pushed into prompts, wrappers, or application-specific orchestration logic, creating hidden interoperability and maintenance costs. To make this gap actionable, we further identify major forms of technical debt in today's protocol ecosystem and distill practical guidance for selecting protocols under different deployment settings. We conclude by outlining a research agenda for interoperable, secure, and semantically robust agent ecosystems that move beyond message passing toward shared understanding.

</details>

### [5] [A Logic of Secrecy on Simplicial Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02673)
*Shanxia Wang*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 该论文在标准单纯形认知模型基础上引入保密层，定义了单纯形保密模型和保密算子S_aφ，建立了多智能体系统中基于局部状态的保密逻辑SSL系统，并证明了其可靠性和完备性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准单纯形模型为多智能体系统提供了知识几何语义，但无法充分捕捉保密性。保密性不能仅依赖普通单纯形知识结构作为新的模态来捕获，这促使引入额外的保密层。

Method: 定义单纯形保密模型，在标准单纯形认知模型基础上增加与局部状态关联的智能体相对保密邻域函数。引入原始保密算子S_aφ，其语义要求智能体a在普通单纯形意义下知道φ，且φ的真值集属于与a当前局部状态关联的指定保密邻域之一。建立SSL系统，通过构造辅助颜色规范模型并嵌入原始纯A-色单纯形保密模型类来证明完备性。

Result: 证明了SSL系统相对于单纯形保密模型类是可靠的。对于真正多智能体情况(|A|≥2)，证明了完备性。该框架提供了基于局部状态、几何基础的单纯形模型保密性描述，并给出了可靠公理化系统，在真正多智能体情况下是完备的。

Conclusion: 该研究成功扩展了单纯形模型以处理保密性，通过引入保密邻域函数和保密算子，建立了多智能体系统中基于局部状态的保密逻辑框架，为保密性提供了几何语义和形式化系统。

Abstract: We develop a logic of secrecy on simplicial models for multi-agent systems. Standard simplicial models provide a geometric semantics for knowledge by representing global states as facets of a chromatic simplicial complex and agents' local states as coloured vertices. However, secrecy cannot in general be captured as a genuinely new modality by relying on the ordinary simplicial knowledge structure alone. This motivates the introduction of an additional secrecy layer.
  To this end, we define \emph{simplicial secrecy models}, which enrich standard simplicial epistemic models with agent-relative secrecy neighborhood functions attached to local states. On this basis, we introduce a primitive secrecy operator $S_a\varphi$. Semantically, $S_a\varphi$ holds when agent $a$ knows $\varphi$ in the ordinary simplicial sense and, moreover, the truth set of $\varphi$ belongs to one of the designated secrecy neighborhoods associated with $a$'s current local state. The clause for secrecy thus combines an ordinary knowledge requirement with an additional local-state-based neighborhood requirement, while the frame condition ensures that designated secrecy events remain non-trivial from the perspective of every other agent.
  We formulate a system $\mathsf{SSL}$ for the resulting language and show that it is sound with respect to the class of simplicial secrecy models. For the genuinely multi-agent case $|A|\ge 2$, we prove completeness by first constructing an auxiliary-colour canonical model and then representing it inside the original class of pure $A$-chromatic simplicial secrecy models. The resulting framework yields a primitive, local-state-based, and geometrically grounded account of secrecy on simplicial models, together with a sound axiomatization and, in the genuinely multi-agent case, a complete one.

</details>


### [6] [Compositionality of Lyapunov functions via assume-guarantee reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03017)
*Matteo Capucci,David Jaz Myers*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 提出基于范畴论的假设-保证推理框架，将系统视为透镜，适用于广义Moore机、部分可观测马尔可夫过程和参数化ODE系统，特别为(L)ISS Lyapunov函数提供了新的假设-保证推理公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为组合模型检查中的假设-保证推理提供统一的范畴论框架，将先前关于广义Moore机组合性的工作扩展到更广泛的系统类型，包括部分可观测马尔可夫过程和参数化ODE系统。

Method: 采用范畴论方法，将系统视为透镜，通过切性（具有截面的纤维化）定义广义Moore机的类型，在切性内部纤维化的2-范畴中构造对称幺半双范畴上认证接线图的对称幺半松散右模。

Result: 建立了范畴自然的、可直接组合的假设-保证推理框架，为广义Moore机、部分可观测马尔可夫过程和参数化ODE系统提供了统一的推理方法，特别针对(L)ISS Lyapunov函数给出了新的假设-保证推理公式。

Conclusion: 该范畴论框架为组合模型检查中的假设-保证推理提供了统一的理论基础，能够适应多种系统类型，并通过2-函子构造实现了良好的组合性，为复杂系统的形式验证提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Assume-guarantee reasoning is a technique for compositional model checking in which system specifications are checked under certain assumptions on system parameters or inputs, and provide guarantees on observations of system state. We present a categorical framework for assume-guarantee reasoning for safety problems by viewing systems as lenses, following our earlier work on the compositionality of generalized Moore machines. Generalized Moore machines include ordinary Moore machines, partially observable Markov (decision) processes, and systems of parameterized ODEs (control systems); our framework gives assume-guarantee reasoning specially adapted to each of these cases. In particular, we give a novel formulation of assume-guarantee reasoning for (local) input-to-state stability ((L)ISS) Lyapunov functions on systems of parameterized ODEs.
  Our framework is categorically natural and straightforwardly compositional. A flavor of generalized Moore machine is determined by a tangency: a fibration with a section. We show that symmetric monoidal loose right modules of assume-guarantee certified generalized Moore machines over symmetric monoidal double categories of certified wiring diagrams can be constructed 2-functorially from fibrations internal to the 2-category of tangencies.

</details>


### [7] [HistMSO: A Logic for Reasoning about Consistency Models with MONA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03085)
*Isabelle Coget,Étienne Lozes*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 提出HistMSO逻辑用于形式化分析复制数据系统的历史和执行，能够表达42种一致性模型中的39种，并通过MONA工具实现自动化推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复制数据系统的一致性模型推理具有挑战性，需要深入理解一致性模型本身以及机械化验证方法中大量的人工输入。现有方法在形式化分析和自动化推理方面存在不足。

Method: 引入HistMSO（历史单子二阶逻辑），用于形式化表示复制数据系统的历史和抽象执行。开发将HistMSO可满足性和模型检测问题归约为字上MSO问题的方法，并利用MONA工具实现自动化推理。

Result: HistMSO能够表达Viotti和Vukolic层次结构中42种一致性模型中的39种。通过归约到字上MSO问题，实现了对一致性模型的自动化推理。

Conclusion: HistMSO为复制数据系统的一致性模型提供了强大的形式化分析框架，能够表达大多数已知一致性模型，并通过自动化工具支持高效推理，显著降低了人工验证负担。

Abstract: Reasoning about consistency models for replicated data systems is a challenging task that requires a deep understanding of both the consistency models themselves and a large part of human inputs in mechanized verification approaches.
  In this work, we introduce an approach to reasoning about consistency models for replicated data systems. We introduce HistMSO, a monadic second-order logic (MSO) for histories and abstract executions, the formal models of executions of replicated data systems introduced by Burckhardt. We show that HistMSO can express 39 out of 42 consistency models from Viotti and Vukolic hierarchy. Moreover, we develop a method for reducing HistMSO satisfiability and model-checking to the same problems for MSO over words. While doing this, we leverage the MONA tool for automated reasoning on consistency models.

</details>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [8] [High Volatility and Action Bias Distinguish LLMs from Humans in Group Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02578)
*Sahaj Singh Maini,Robert L. Goldstone,Zoran Tiganj*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该研究比较了人类与大型语言模型在群体协调任务中的表现差异，发现人类能适应并稳定行为，而LLMs则表现出过度切换且难以改进


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型能力增强，需要探究它们是否具备与人类相当的群体协调能力，以及是否使用相似的协调策略，以理解LLMs在群体协作方面的局限性

Method: 使用群体二分搜索游戏作为实验平台，这是一个具有共同利益和不完全监控的n人游戏。参与者独立提交数值以共同达到随机分配的目标数字，通过群体反馈迭代调整，比较人类和LLMs在协调任务中的表现

Result: 人类能够适应并稳定行为，而LLMs往往无法在多个游戏中改进，表现出过度切换行为，这损害了群体收敛。更丰富的反馈（如数值误差幅度）对人类有显著帮助，但对LLMs影响很小

Conclusion: 通过基于人类基线和机制层面指标的分析，揭示了人类与LLM群体在协调能力上的差异，为缩小协调差距提供了行为基础的诊断方法

Abstract: Humans exhibit remarkable abilities to coordinate in groups. As large language models (LLMs) become more capable, it remains an open question whether they can demonstrate comparable adaptive coordination and whether they use the same strategies as humans. To investigate this, we compare LLM and human performance on a common-interest game with imperfect monitoring: Group Binary Search. In this n-player game, participants need to coordinate their actions to achieve a common objective. Players independently submit numerical values in an effort to collectively sum to a randomly assigned target number. Without direct communication, they rely on group feedback to iteratively adjust their submissions until they reach the target number. Our findings show that, unlike humans who adapt and stabilize their behavior over time, LLMs often fail to improve across games and exhibit excessive switching, which impairs group convergence. Moreover, richer feedback (e.g., numerical error magnitude) benefits humans substantially but has small effects on LLMs. Taken together, by grounding the analysis in human baselines and mechanism-level metrics, including reactivity scaling, switching dynamics, and learning across games, we point to differences in human and LLM groups and provide a behaviorally grounded diagnostic for closing the coordination gap.

</details>


### [9] [Do Agent Societies Develop Intellectual Elites? The Hidden Power Laws of Collective Cognition in LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02674)
*Kavana Venkatesh,Jiaming Cui*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文首次对LLM多智能体系统中的协调动力学进行了大规模实证研究，揭示了协调遵循重尾级联、通过优先连接集中到智力精英、以及随着系统规模增长产生更频繁极端事件的三个耦合定律，并提出了解决集成瓶颈的DTI方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统作为交互智能体社会被广泛部署，但系统扩展往往导致收益递减或不稳定，其原因尚不清楚。需要理解多智能体系统中的协调动力学，以解决可扩展性问题。

Method: 提出原子事件级表述，将推理重构为协调级联。分析了超过150万次交互，涵盖不同任务、拓扑和规模。引入赤字触发集成(DTI)方法，在失衡时选择性地增加集成。

Result: 发现了三个耦合定律：协调遵循重尾级联分布；通过优先连接集中到智力精英；随着系统规模增长，极端事件频率增加。揭示了集成瓶颈机制：协调扩展随系统规模增长，但整合不增长，导致大规模但弱整合的推理过程。DTI方法在协调失败时能提高性能，同时不抑制大规模推理。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了集体认知的定量定律，并确定协调结构作为理解和改进可扩展多智能体智能的基本、先前未测量的维度。集成瓶颈是限制多智能体系统扩展的关键机制，DTI为解决这一问题提供了有效方法。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) multi-agent systems are increasingly deployed as interacting agent societies, yet scaling these systems often yields diminishing or unstable returns, the causes of which remain poorly understood. We present the first large-scale empirical study of coordination dynamics in LLM-based multi-agent systems, introducing an atomic event-level formulation that reconstructs reasoning as cascades of coordination. Analyzing over 1.5 Million interactions across tasks, topologies, and scales, we uncover three coupled laws: coordination follows heavy-tailed cascades, concentrates via preferential attachment into intellectual elites, and produces increasingly frequent extreme events as system size grows. We show that these effects are coupled through a single structural mechanism: an integration bottleneck, in which coordination expansion scales with system size while consolidation does not, producing large but weakly integrated reasoning processes. To test this mechanism, we introduce Deficit-Triggered Integration (DTI), which selectively increases integration under imbalance. DTI improves performance precisely where coordination fails, without suppressing large-scale reasoning. Together, our results establish quantitative laws of collective cognition and identify coordination structure as a fundamental, previously unmeasured axis for understanding and improving scalable multi-agent intelligence.

</details>


### [10] [Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning-based Joint Design of Low-Carbon P2P Market and Bidding Strategy in Microgrids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02728)
*Junhao Ren,Honglin Gao,Sijie Wang,Lan Zhao,Qiyu Kang,Aniq Ashan,Yajuan Sun,Gaoxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出基于多智能体强化学习的微电网社区日内P2P交易框架，平衡个体经济收益与社区低碳排放目标


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可再生能源发电的不确定性和实时市场的不稳定性限制了清洁能源在微电网社区中的有效利用。现有P2P和微电网协调方法通常依赖集中式优化或限制性协调规则，难以在实际应用中实施。

Method: 将微电网决策过程建模为去中心化部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程，采用多智能体强化学习框架求解。引入新型市场出清机制进行宏观调控，激励微电网优先消纳本地可再生能源。

Result: 仿真结果表明，自利投标策略与P2P市场设计的结合显著提高了可再生能源利用率，减少了对高碳排放外部电力的依赖。框架实现了本地自治、自利追求与社区层面经济环境效益的平衡整合。

Conclusion: 该框架为微电网社区提供了一种可行的日内P2P交易解决方案，能够在保障个体自主决策的同时，通过市场机制实现社区层面的低碳排放目标，促进可再生能源的有效利用。

Abstract: The challenges of the uncertainties in renewable energy generation and the instability of the real-time market limit the effective utilization of clean energy in microgrid communities. Existing peer-to-peer (P2P) and microgrid coordination approaches typically rely on certain centralized optimization or restrictive coordination rules which are difficult to be implemented in real-life applications. To address the challenge, we propose an intraday P2P trading framework that allows self-interested microgrids to pursue their economic benefits, while allowing the market operator to maximize the social welfare, namely the low carbon emission objective, of the entire community. Specifically, the decision-making processes of the microgrids are formulated as a Decentralized Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (DEC-POMDP) and solved using a Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) framework. Such an approach grants each microgrid a high degree of decision-making autonomy, while a novel market clearing mechanism is introduced to provide macro-regulation, incentivizing microgrids to prioritize local renewable energy consumption and hence reduce carbon emissions. Simulation results demonstrate that the combination of the self-interested bidding strategy and the P2P market design helps significantly improve renewable energy utilization and reduce reliance on external electricity with high carbon-emissions. The framework achieves a balanced integration of local autonomy, self-interest pursuit, and improved community-level economic and environmental benefits.

</details>


### [11] [Fully Byzantine-Resilient Distributed Multi-Agent Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02791)
*Haejoon Lee,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出一种能抵御拜占庭边攻击的分布式多智能体Q学习算法，确保所有智能体的价值函数几乎必然收敛到最优值


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弹性MARL方法通常只能保证几乎必然收敛到接近最优的价值函数，或需要限制性假设才能收敛到最优解，导致智能体可能无法学习最优策略

Method: 提出基于冗余的过滤机制，利用两跳邻居信息验证传入消息，同时保持双向信息流；引入新的拓扑条件并给出系统化构建方法

Result: 算法在拜占庭边攻击下仍能保证所有智能体价值函数几乎必然收敛到最优值；新拓扑条件可在多项式时间内验证；仿真验证优于现有方法

Conclusion: 提出的分布式Q学习算法能有效抵御拜占庭边攻击，确保收敛到最优解，解决了现有方法在攻击下无法学习最优策略的问题

Abstract: We study Byzantine-resilient distributed multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), where agents must collaboratively learn optimal value functions over a compromised communication network. Existing resilient MARL approaches typically guarantee almost sure convergence only to near-optimal value functions, or require restrictive assumptions to ensure convergence to optimal solution. As a result, agents may fail to learn the optimal policies under these methods. To address this, we propose a novel distributed Q-learning algorithm, under which all agents' value functions converge almost surely to the optimal value functions despite Byzantine edge attacks. The key idea is a redundancy-based filtering mechanism that leverages two-hop neighbor information to validate incoming messages, while preserving bidirectional information flow. We then introduce a new topological condition for the convergence of our algorithm, present a systematic method to construct such networks, and prove that this condition can be verified in polynomial time. We validate our results through simulations, showing that our method converges to the optimal solutions, whereas prior methods fail under Byzantine edge attacks.

</details>

*Zoe Paraskevopoulou,Anja Petković Komel,Sophie Rain,Lefteris Lazaropoulos,Alexis Terry*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文档提供了act规范和验证语言的形式化定义与元理论，包括语法、操作指针语义、类型系统及主要元理论结果（类型安全性）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为act规范和验证语言提供完整的形式化基础，确保其语义精确性、类型安全性，并为后续工具实现和验证提供理论依据。

Method: 采用形式化方法：1) 定义语言的语法结构；2) 建立操作指针语义模型；3) 设计类型系统；4) 证明类型安全性等元理论性质。

Result: 完成了act语言的形式化规范，包括：语法定义、语义模型、类型系统，并证明了类型安全性等关键元理论结果。

Conclusion: 该技术报告为act规范和验证语言建立了坚实的形式化基础，确保了语言的语义一致性和类型安全性，为实际应用和工具开发提供了理论保障。

Abstract: This technical report contains the formal definitions and metatheory for the act specification and verification language. It documents the syntax, the operational pointer semantics, the type system and the main metatheoretic results (type-safety).

</details>

### [13] [Backdoor Attacks on Decentralised Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02372)
*Oğuzhan Ersoy,Nikolay Blagoev,Jona te Lintelo,Stefanos Koffas,Marina Krček,Stjepan Picek*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 首次提出针对流水线并行训练的大型语言模型的后门攻击方法，攻击者仅控制流水线中间阶段即可成功注入后门，使模型对齐率从80%降至6%，且安全对齐训练后仍有60%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化后训练虽然利用数据和流水线并行技术提高效率，但容易受到恶意参与者的中毒和后门攻击。现有研究主要关注数据并行或联邦学习的攻击防御，而对流水线并行的研究仅限于中毒攻击，缺乏对后门攻击的探索。

Method: 提出首个针对流水线并行的后门攻击方法，攻击者仅控制流水线中间阶段而非整个模型或数据集。通过精心设计的触发机制，在模型后训练过程中注入后门，使模型在特定触发词出现时产生错误对齐。

Result: 实验结果显示，攻击者仅控制流水线中间阶段即可成功注入后门，使模型对齐率从80%降至6%。即使对最终模型应用安全对齐训练，后门攻击在60%的情况下仍能成功，证明攻击具有强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 流水线并行训练存在严重安全漏洞，即使攻击者仅控制中间阶段也能成功实施后门攻击。现有防御措施如安全对齐训练无法完全消除此类威胁，需要开发专门针对流水线并行架构的安全机制。

Abstract: Decentralised post-training of large language models utilises data and pipeline parallelism techniques to split the data and the model. Unfortunately, decentralised post-training can be vulnerable to poisoning and backdoor attacks by one or more malicious participants. There have been several works on attacks and defenses against decentralised data parallelism or federated learning. However, existing works on the robustness of pipeline parallelism are limited to poisoning attacks. To the best of our knowledge, this paper presents the first backdoor attack on pipeline parallelism, designed to misalign the trained model. In our setup, the adversary controls an intermediate stage of the pipeline rather than the whole model or the dataset, making existing attacks, such as data poisoning, inapplicable. Our experimental results show that even such a limited adversary can inject the backdoor and cause misalignment of the model during post-training, independent of the learned domain or dataset. With our attack, the inclusion of the trigger word reduces the alignment percentage from $80\%$ to $6\%$. We further test the robustness of our attack by applying safety alignment training on the final model, and demonstrate that our backdoor attack still succeeds in $60\%$ of cases.

</details>


### [14] [Automated Malware Family Classification using Weighted Hierarchical Ensembles of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02490)
*Samita Bai,Hamed Jelodar,Tochukwu Emmanuel Nwankwo,Parisa Hamedi,Mohammad Meymani,Roozbeh Razavi-Far,Ali A. Ghorbani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于加权层次集成预训练大语言模型的零标签恶意软件家族分类框架，不依赖特征学习或模型重训练，通过决策级预测聚合实现开放世界场景下的恶意软件分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有恶意软件家族分类方法依赖标注数据集、手工特征、监督训练或动态分析，在混淆、加壳和快速演变的真实世界威胁中可扩展性和有效性受限，特别是在开放世界场景下。

Method: 基于加权层次集成预训练大语言模型，不进行特征级学习或模型重训练，而是聚合多个具有互补推理能力的LLMs的决策级预测，使用经验推导的宏F1分数加权，并按层次组织：先解决粗粒度恶意行为，再分配细粒度恶意软件家族。

Result: 该方法增强了鲁棒性，减少了个体模型的不稳定性，并与分析师风格推理保持一致，为开放世界场景下的恶意软件家族分类提供了更有效的解决方案。

Conclusion: 提出的零标签恶意软件家族分类框架通过加权层次集成预训练LLMs，克服了现有方法的局限性，在真实世界恶意软件分析中具有更好的可扩展性和有效性。

Abstract: Malware family classification remains a challenging task in automated malware analysis, particularly in real-world settings characterized by obfuscation, packing, and rapidly evolving threats. Existing machine learning and deep learning approaches typically depend on labeled datasets, handcrafted features, supervised training, or dynamic analysis, which limits their scalability and effectiveness in open-world scenarios.
  This paper presents a zero-label malware family classification framework based on a weighted hierarchical ensemble of pretrained large language models (LLMs). Rather than relying on feature-level learning or model retraining, the proposed approach aggregates decision-level predictions from multiple LLMs with complementary reasoning strengths. Model outputs are weighted using empirically derived macro-F1 scores and organized hierarchically, first resolving coarse-grained malicious behavior before assigning fine-grained malware families. This structure enhances robustness, reduces individual model instability, and aligns with analyst-style reasoning.

</details>


### [15] [From Theory to Practice: Code Generation Using LLMs for CAPEC and CWE Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02548)
*Murtuza Shahzad,Joseph Wilson,Ibrahim Al Azher,Hamed Alhoori,Mona Rahimi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究人员利用GPT-4o、Llama和Claude等大语言模型生成与CAPEC和CWE描述对应的漏洞代码片段数据集，包含Java、Python和JavaScript三种语言的615个代码示例，用于漏洞检测和修复研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件漏洞数据集通常缺乏与特定漏洞描述明确关联的详细代码片段，这限制了高级研究的开展，也阻碍了对安全漏洞的深入理解。需要更全面、详细的漏洞代码资源来支持机器学习模型训练和漏洞检测系统开发。

Method: 利用GPT-4o、Llama和Claude等生成式预训练Transformer模型，基于CAPEC和CWE文档中的漏洞描述生成相应的代码片段。通过三种不同模型生成代码以确保多样性和可靠性。

Result: 生成了包含615个CAPEC代码片段的数据集，涵盖Java、Python和JavaScript三种编程语言。三种模型生成的代码具有高度一致性（余弦相似度0.98），初步评估显示数据集具有高准确性，可作为漏洞识别系统的可靠参考。

Conclusion: 该数据集不仅增强了代码中安全漏洞的理解，还为专注于自动漏洞检测和修复的机器学习模型训练提供了宝贵资源。大语言模型生成的漏洞代码数据集在准确性和多样性方面表现出色，是该领域最广泛和多样化的资源之一。

Abstract: The increasing complexity and volume of software systems have heightened the importance of identifying and mitigating security vulnerabilities. The existing software vulnerability datasets frequently fall short in providing comprehensive, detailed code snippets explicitly linked to specific vulnerability descriptions, reducing their utility for advanced research and hindering efforts to develop a deeper understanding of security vulnerabilities. To address this challenge, we present a novel dataset that provides examples of vulnerable code snippets corresponding to Common Attack Pattern Enumerations and Classifications (CAPEC) and Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) descriptions. By employing the capabilities of Generative Pre-trained Transformer (GPT) models, we have developed a robust methodology for generating these examples. Our approach utilizes GPT-4o, Llama and Claude models to generate code snippets that exhibit specific vulnerabilities as described in CAPEC and CWE documentation. This dataset not only enhances the understanding of security vulnerabilities in code but also serves as a valuable resource for training machine learning models focused on automatic vulnerability detection and remediation. Preliminary evaluations suggest that the dataset generated by Large Language Models demonstrates high accuracy and can serve as a reliable reference for vulnerability identification systems. We found consistent results across the three models, with 0.98 cosine similarity among codes. The final dataset comprises 615 CAPEC code snippets in three programming languages: Java, Python, and JavaScript, making it one of the most extensive and diverse resources in this domain.

</details>


### [16] [The Quantum-Cryptographic Co-evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02591)
*Ashish Kundu,Ramana Kompella*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出二维坐标系分析密码学演进，横轴为密码弹性，纵轴为计算能力，通过四个象限分类从经典系统到量子安全架构的过渡，强调"量子鸿沟"是最大系统性风险


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子计算向密码相关量子计算机(CRQC)发展，全球密码基础设施面临生存威胁。需要系统分析密码弹性与计算能力的协同演进，识别过渡过程中的风险

Method: 引入二维坐标系：x轴为密码弹性(从经典到量子安全)，y轴为计算能力(从经典到量子)。通过分析四个象限，分类从传统经典系统到量子安全架构的过渡路径

Result: 识别出"量子鸿沟"——CRQC实现与量子安全技术采用之间的时间差，代表最高系统性风险。需要立即转向密码敏捷框架以应对这一风险

Conclusion: 量子计算对密码基础设施构成重大威胁，必须通过密码敏捷框架加速向量子安全架构过渡，以弥合量子鸿沟并确保系统安全

Abstract: As quantum computing matures toward the realization of Cryptographically Relevant Quantum Computers (CRQC), global cryptographic infrastructure faces an existential threat. This paper introduces a two-dimensional coordinate system to map the co-evolution of cryptographic resilience (x-axis) and computational capability (y-axis). By analyzing the four resulting quadrants, we categorize the transition from legacy classical systems to quantum-resilient architectures. We argue that the "Quantum Gap" - the delta between CRQC arrival and quantum-safe adoption represents the highest systemic risk, necessitating an immediate transition to crypto-agile frameworks.

</details>


### [17] [ContractShield: Bridging Semantic-Structural Gaps via Hierarchical Cross-Modal Fusion for Multi-Label Vulnerability Detection in Obfuscated Smart Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02771)
*Minh-Dai Tran-Duong,Nguyen Hai Phong,Nguyen Chi Thanh,Doan Minh Trung,Tram Truong-Huu,Van-Hau Pham,Phan The Duy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ContractShield：一种用于智能合约漏洞检测的鲁棒多模态框架，通过三级融合机制有效关联语义、时序和结构特征，在对抗性混淆条件下保持高检测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能合约漏洞检测方法面临两个主要问题：1）攻击者使用虚假代码注入和控制流操纵等混淆技术来逃避检测；2）现有多模态方法通常孤立处理不同特征，并使用简单的拼接融合策略，忽略了跨模态交互，导致单个模态的混淆会显著降低检测精度。

Method: 提出ContractShield框架，包含特征提取和三级融合机制：1）特征提取：使用CodeBERT+滑动窗口捕获源代码语义依赖，xLSTM建模操作码序列的时序动态，GATv2识别控制流图中的结构不变性；2）三级融合：自注意力识别各特征空间内的漏洞模式，跨模态注意力建立跨模态互补信号的有意义连接，自适应加权根据混淆条件下的可靠性动态校准特征贡献。

Result: 实验评估表明ContractShield具有鲁棒性：在混淆数据上获得89%的汉明分数，仅比非混淆数据下降1-3个百分点；同时检测五种主要漏洞类型，F1分数达到91%，在对抗条件下比最先进方法高出6-15个百分点。

Conclusion: ContractShield通过创新的三级融合机制有效关联多模态特征，显著提升了智能合约漏洞检测在对抗性混淆条件下的鲁棒性和准确性，为解决现有方法的局限性提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Smart contracts are increasingly targeted by adversaries employing obfuscation techniques such as bogus code injection and control flow manipulation to evade vulnerability detection. Existing multimodal methods often process semantic, temporal, and structural features in isolation and fuse them using simple strategies such as concatenation, which neglects cross-modal interactions and weakens robustness, as obfuscation of a single modality can sharply degrade detection accuracy. To address these challenges, we propose ContractShield, a robust multimodal framework with a novel fusion mechanism that effectively correlates multiple complementary features through a three-level fusion. Self-attention first identifies patterns that indicate vulnerability within each feature space. Cross-modal attention then establishes meaningful connections between complementary signals across modalities. Then, adaptive weighting dynamically calibrates feature contributions based on their reliability under obfuscation. For feature extraction, ContractShield integrates (1) CodeBERT with a sliding window mechanism to capture semantic dependencies in source code, (2) Extended long short-term memory (xLSTM) to model temporal dynamics in opcode sequences, and (3) GATv2 to identify structural invariants in control flow graphs (CFGs) that remain stable across obfuscation. Empirical evaluation demonstrates resilience of ContractShield, achieving a 89 percentage Hamming Score with only a 1-3 percentage drop compared to non-obfuscated data. The framework simultaneously detects five major vulnerability types with 91 percentage F1-score, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches by 6-15 percentage under adversarial conditions.

</details>


### [18] [Open Challenges for Secure and Scalable Wi-Fi Connectivity in Rural Areas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02774)
*Philip Virgil Berrer Astillo,Jayasree Sengupta,Mathy Vanhoef*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文分析了农村地区付费Wi-Fi热点的安全漏洞，通过菲律宾和印度的实地调查发现普遍存在的安全问题，并演示了两种实际攻击方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农村地区可靠、经济、安全的互联网连接仍面临挑战，付费Wi-Fi热点作为可扩展解决方案正在兴起，但其安全特性尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 1. 在菲律宾和印度进行Wi-Fi调查，了解实际部署情况；2. 对部署进行安全评估，演示两种实际攻击：劫持他人付费连接和建立恶意热点；3. 分析漏洞根源，引入针对性威胁模型。

Result: 1. 菲律宾农村地区Piso-WiFi热点广泛存在；2. 印度PM-WANI倡议逐渐获得关注；3. 发现并验证了两种实际攻击漏洞；4. 提出了安全改进方案，包括安全缓存架构。

Conclusion: 新兴农村连接基础设施存在重大安全挑战，需要针对性的安全改进措施，论文为更安全、可扩展的部署提供了方向。

Abstract: Providing reliable, affordable, and secure Internet connectivity in rural areas remains a major challenge. Pay-for-use Wi-Fi hotspots are emerging as a scalable solution to provide affordable Internet access in underserved and rural regions. Despite their growing adoption, their security properties remain largely unexplored. In this paper, we present a security analysis of these hotspot ecosystems based on Wi-Fi surveys and practical attack validation. We first perform a Wi-Fi survey conducted in two countries, namely the Philippines and India, to understand the deployment and adoption of such systems in practice. Our results suggest that Piso-WiFi pay-to-use hotspots are particularly widespread in rural regions of the Philippines, and that India's PM-WANI initiative is slowly gaining traction. We then perform a security assessment of these deployments and demonstrate two practical attacks: hijacking another user's paid connection; and rogue hotspots. We analyze the root causes of these vulnerabilities, introduce threat models tailored to pay-for-use hotspot deployments, and outline practical security improvements, including a secure caching architecture. Our findings highlight security challenges in emerging rural connectivity infrastructure and provide directions toward more secure and scalable deployments.

</details>


### [19] [Towards Secure Agent Skills: Architecture, Threat Taxonomy, and Security Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02837)
*Zhiyuan Li,Jingzheng Wu,Xiang Ling,Xing Cui,Tianyue Luo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文对Agent Skills框架进行了首次全面安全分析，识别了其生命周期四个阶段的安全攻击面，构建了威胁分类法，并通过真实安全事件验证，发现最严重威胁源于框架本身的结构性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent Skills作为一种新兴的开放标准，已在多个智能体平台快速采用并形成大型社区市场，但其安全属性尚未得到系统研究。随着该框架的广泛部署，理解其安全风险变得至关重要。

Method: 1. 定义Agent Skill的完整生命周期（创建、分发、部署、执行四个阶段）并识别每个阶段的结构性攻击面；2. 构建包含7个类别和17个场景的威胁分类法，基于架构分析和现实证据；3. 通过分析Agent Skills生态系统中的5个已确认安全事件验证分类法。

Result: 分析揭示了最严重的威胁源于框架本身的结构性特性：缺乏数据-指令边界、单一审批的持久信任模型、以及缺乏强制性的市场安全审查。这些威胁无法仅通过增量缓解措施解决。研究提供了每个威胁类别的防御方向、开放研究挑战和可操作建议。

Conclusion: Agent Skills框架存在严重的安全风险，主要源于其架构设计缺陷。需要系统性安全改进而非渐进式修补，包括重新设计信任模型、建立数据-指令分离机制、实施强制安全审查等根本性变革。

Abstract: Agent Skills is an emerging open standard that defines a modular, filesystem-based packaging format enabling LLM-based agents to acquire domain-specific expertise on demand. Despite rapid adoption across multiple agentic platforms and the emergence of large community marketplaces, the security properties of Agent Skills have not been systematically studied. This paper presents the first comprehensive security analysis of the Agent Skills framework. We define the full lifecycle of an Agent Skill across four phases -- Creation, Distribution, Deployment, and Execution -- and identify the structural attack surface each phase introduces. Building on this lifecycle analysis, we construct a threat taxonomy comprising seven categories and seventeen scenarios organized across three attack layers, grounded in both architectural analysis and real-world evidence. We validate the taxonomy through analysis of five confirmed security incidents in the Agent Skills ecosystem. Based on these findings, we discuss defense directions for each threat category, identify open research challenges, and provide actionable recommendations for stakeholders. Our analysis reveals that the most severe threats arise from structural properties of the framework itself, including the absence of a data-instruction boundary, a single-approval persistent trust model, and the lack of mandatory marketplace security review, and cannot be addressed through incremental mitigations alone.

</details>


### [20] [Analyzing Healthcare Interoperability Vulnerabilities: Formal Modeling and Graph-Theoretic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03043)
*Jawad Mohammed,Gahangir Hossain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出FHIR资源访问图（FRAG）模型，用于检测医疗FHIR平台中的并发竞争条件，相比时间窗口基线方法在完全并发访问下F1分数提升64.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗FHIR平台支持对共享患者资源的并发异步访问，但FHIR规范缺乏并发控制协议，现有研究仅关注操作系统内核的竞争条件检测或FHIR的认证和注入攻击，将并发访问视为顺序处理，存在研究空白。

Method: 引入FHIR资源访问图（FRAG）形式化模型G=(P,R,E,λ,τ,S)，其中节点表示并发进程，带类型边表示资源访问事件，竞争条件表示为可检测的结构属性。定义了三种临床相关竞争条件类：同时写入冲突（SWC）、TOCTOU授权违规（TAV）和级联更新竞争（CUR）。实现为三遍图遍历检测算法，在1,500个合成FHIR R4事务日志上进行测试。

Result: 在完全并发访问（C2）条件下，FRAG达到90.0%的F1分数，而时间窗口基线方法仅为25.5%，提升了64.5个百分点。

Conclusion: FRAG模型能有效检测FHIR平台中的并发竞争条件，填补了该领域的研究空白，为医疗信息系统的并发安全提供了重要工具。

Abstract: In a healthcare environment, the healthcare interoperability platforms based on HL7 FHIR allow concurrent, asynchronous access to a set of shared patient resources, which are independent systems, i.e., EHR systems, pharmacy systems, lab systems, and devices. The FHIR specification lacks a protocol for concurrency control, and the research on detecting a race condition only targets the OS kernel. The research on FHIR security only targets authentication and injection attacks, considering concurrent access to patient resources to be sequential. The gap in the research in this area is addressed through the introduction of FHIR Resource Access Graph (FRAG), a formally defined graph G = (P,R,E, λ, τ, S), in which the nodes are the concurrent processes, the typed edges represent the resource access events, and the race conditions are represented as detectable structural properties. Three clinically relevant race condition classes are formally specified: Simultaneous Write Conflict (SWC), TOCTOU Authorization Violation (TAV), and Cascading Update Race (CUR). The FRAG model is implemented as a three-pass graph traversal detection algorithm and tested against a time window-based baseline on 1,500 synthetic FHIR R4 transaction logs. Under full concurrent access (C2), FRAG attains a 90.0% F1 score vs. 25.5% for the baseline, a 64.5 pp improvement.

</details>

### [21] [Computing with Living Neurons: Chaos-Controlled Reservoir Computing with Knowledge Transplant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02552)
*Seung Hyun Kim,Zhi Dou,Gaurav Upadhyay,Anay Pattanaik,Leo Maslov,Lav Varshney,John Beggs,Howard Gritton,Mattia Gazzola*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出混沌控制储层计算(cc-RC)用于活体神经培养，通过混沌控制稳定神经活动，结合知识移植(KT)实现跨神经底物的模型复用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用活体神经培养的动态丰富特性进行自适应计算，但面临生物内在变异性和活动不稳定性的挑战，需要开发能够稳定神经活动并实现跨样本知识共享的方法

Method: cc-RC包含三个步骤：1)预训练识别每个培养物的动态特征和相图吸引子；2)低功率光学混沌控制稳定自发和刺激诱发活动；3)在控制机制下训练读出层。KT方法将专家培养物学到的储层映射移植到吸引子等效的学生培养物

Result: 在数百个神经样本上，cc-RC实现了稳健学习和模式分类，准确率和模型寿命比标准RC提高约300%。KT将训练时间缩短至分钟级别，同时提高性能

Conclusion: cc-RC和KT方法能够实现跨神经底物的可复用学习模型，支持知识积累和跨神经群体共享，超越了生物寿命限制，为活体神经计算系统开辟了新途径

Abstract: We introduce chaos-controlled Reservoir Computing (cc-RC) for living neural cultures: dynamically rich substrates of unique potential for adaptive computation. To account for intrinsic biological variability, cc-RC combines: (i) pre-training identification of each culture's dynamical signature and phase-portrait attractor; (ii) low-power optical chaos control to stabilize spontaneous and stimulus-evoked activity; (iii) readout training within this controlled regime. Across hundreds of neural samples, cc-RC enables robust learning and pattern classification, improving both accuracy and model longevity by approximately 300% over standard RC. We further propose Knowledge Transplant (KT), for which the reservoir map learned by an expert culture is transplanted to an attractor-equivalent student culture, reducing training time to minutes while improving performance. By enabling cross-substrate, reusable learned models, KT paves the way for knowledge accumulation and sharing across neural populations, transcending biological lifespan limits.

</details>


### [22] [Frame Theoretical Derivation of Three Factor Learning Rule for Oja's Subspace Rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02849)
*Taiki Yamada*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 从Oja子空间规则通过框架理论系统推导出EGHR-PCA三因子学习规则


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为生物学上合理的EGHR-PCA学习规则提供原则性、非启发式的数学推导，将其与数学上的规范对应物（Oja子空间规则）联系起来

Method: 使用框架理论，将Oja子空间规则在对称矩阵空间上相对于自然框架展开，从而系统推导出EGHR-PCA

Result: EGHR-PCA中的全局第三因子恰好作为框架系数出现，该规则在高斯输入下等价于Oja子空间规则

Conclusion: 通过框架理论为生物学上合理的学习规则提供了严格的数学基础，建立了生物可塑性规则与规范数学形式之间的直接联系

Abstract: We show that the error-gated Hebbian rule for PCA (EGHR-PCA), a three-factor learning rule equivalent to Oja's subspace rule under Gaussian inputs, can be systematically derived from Oja's subspace rule using frame theory. The global third factor in EGHR-PCA arises exactly as a frame coefficient when the learning rule is expanded with respect to a natural frame on the space of symmetric matrices. This provides a principled, non-heuristic derivation of a biologically plausible learning rule from its mathematically canonical counterpart.

</details>


### [23] [Accelerating Black-Box Bilevel Optimization with Rank-Based Upper-Level Value Function Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02888)
*Marc Ong,Youhei Akimoto*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出一种基于排序不变性的高效双层优化框架，通过近似上层目标函数排序来避免下层优化完全收敛，显著降低计算成本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统演化方法解决双层优化问题时计算成本高昂，特别是对于具有多模态和变量间强交互作用的复杂优化场景，现有启发式方法缺乏通用性

Method: 利用基于排序的演化算法对单调变换的不变性，直接近似上层目标函数排序，绕过下层优化器的完全收敛要求，采用CMA-ES作为优化器处理连续双层优化问题

Result: 在标准基准测试中表现出竞争力，能够解决先前方法难以处理的多模态和强变量交互问题，显著降低计算负担

Conclusion: 提出的基于排序不变性的框架为双层优化提供了一种高效解决方案，特别适用于复杂优化场景，通过避免下层完全收敛显著提升了计算效率

Abstract: Bilevel optimization is a field of significant theoretical and practical interest, yet solving such optimization problems remains challenging. Evolutionary methods have been employed to address these problems in the black-box setting; however, they incur high computational cost due to the nested nature of bilevel optimization. Although previous methods have attempted to reduce this cost through various heuristic techniques, such approaches limit versatility on challenging optimization landscapes, such as those with multimodality and significant interaction between upper- and lower-level decision variables. In this study, we propose an efficient framework that exploits the invariance of rank-based evolutionary algorithms to monotonic transformations, thereby reducing the computational burden of the lower-level optimization loop. Specifically, our method directly approximates the rankings of the upper-level value function, bypassing the need to run the lower-level optimizer until convergence for each upper-level iteration. We apply this framework to the setting where both levels are continuous, adopting CMA-ES as the optimizer. We demonstrate that our method achieves competitive performance on standard bilevel optimization benchmarks and can solve problems that are intractable with previously proposed methods, particularly those with multimodality and strong inter-variable interactions.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [24] [Making Written Theorems Explorable by Grounding Them in Formal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02598)
*Hita Kambhamettu,Will Crichton,Sean Welleck,Harrison Goldstein,Andrew Head*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出"可探索定理"系统，利用LLM将数学定理和证明转化为Lean形式化语言，实现交互式证明理解，超越静态文本限制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的解释虽然能提高技术内容可访问性，但静态文本输出无法支持交互式执行和逐步调试。需要将解释基于形式化表示以实现超越静态文本的交互功能

Method: 开发"可探索定理"系统：1) 使用LLM将定理及其书面证明翻译为Lean（机器检查证明的编程语言）；2) 将书面证明与Lean代码链接；3) 支持逐步粒度证明、自定义示例/反例测试、逻辑依赖追踪；4) 通过执行Lean证明并提取中间状态生成每个步骤

Result: 用户研究（n=16）显示：在证明阅读任务中，使用可探索功能的参与者能给出更好、更正确、更详细的答案，表现出对底层数学更强的整体理解

Conclusion: 将LLM解释基于形式化表示（如Lean）能实现超越静态文本的交互功能，显著提升数学证明理解效果，为技术内容可访问性提供新方向

Abstract: LLM-generated explanations can make technical content more accessible, but there is a ceiling on what they can support interactively. Because LLM outputs are static text, they cannot be executed or stepped through. We argue that grounding explanations in a formalized representation enables interactive affordances beyond what static text supports. We instantiate this idea for mathematical proof comprehension with explorable theorems, a system that uses LLMs to translate a theorem and its written proof into Lean, a programming language for machine-checked proofs, and links the written proof with the Lean code. Readers can work through the proof at a step-level granularity, test custom examples or counterexamples, and trace the logical dependencies bridging each step. Each worked-out step is produced by executing the Lean proof on that example and extracting its intermediate state. A user study ($n = 16$) shows potential advantages of this approach: in a proof-reading task, participants who had access to the provided explorability features gave better, more correct, and more detailed answers to comprehension questions, demonstrating a stronger overall understanding of the underlying mathematics.

</details>


### [25] [LitPivot: Developing Well-Situated Research Ideas Through Dynamic Contextualization and Critique within the Literature Landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02600)
*Hita Kambhamettu,Bhavana Dalvi Mishra,Andrew Head,Jonathan Bragg,Aakanksha Naik,Joseph Chee Chang,Pao Siangliulue*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: LitPivot：一种支持文献与创意动态交互的研究工具，通过文献引导的转向机制帮助研究者迭代完善研究想法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究工具存在局限性：文献工具仅帮助理解固定文献集，而创意工具则基于静态预选论文评估想法。缺乏支持文献与创意动态交互的工具，即当想法改变时相关文献也会随之变化，反之亦然。

Method: 提出"文献引导的转向"机制，开发LitPivot工具。研究者可同时起草和验证想法，系统动态检索与想法特定部分相关的论文集群，并提供基于文献的批判性建议以指导想法修订。

Result: 实验室研究（n=17）显示研究者能产生评分更高的想法，并自我报告对文献空间有更强的理解；开放式研究（n=5）揭示了研究者如何使用LitPivot迭代演化自己的想法。

Conclusion: LitPivot通过支持文献与创意之间的动态交互，有效解决了研究创意开发中的关键挑战，帮助研究者产生更高质量的想法并深化对文献领域的理解。

Abstract: Developing a novel research idea is hard. It must be distinct enough from prior work to claim a contribution while also building on it. This requires iteratively reviewing literature and refining an idea based on what a researcher reads; yet when an idea changes, the literature that matters often changes with it. Most tools offer limited support for this interplay: literature tools help researchers understand a fixed body of work, while ideation tools evaluate ideas against a static, pre-curated set of papers. We introduce literature-initiated pivots, a mechanism where engagement with literature prompts revision to a developing idea, and where that revision changes which literature is relevant. We operationalize this in LitPivot, where researchers concurrently draft and vet an idea. LitPivot dynamically retrieves clusters of papers relevant to a selected part of the idea and proposes literature-informed critiques for how to revise it. A lab study ($n{=}17$) shows researchers produced higher-rated ideas with stronger self-reported understanding of the literature space; an open-ended study ($n{=}5$) reveals how researchers use LitPivot to iteratively evolve their own ideas.

</details>


### [26] [Toys that listen, talk, and play: Understanding Children's Sensemaking and Interactions with AI Toys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02629)
*Aayushi Dangol,Meghna Gupta,Daeun Yoo,Robert Wolfe,Jason Yip,Franziska Roesner,Julie A. Kientz*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 儿童与AI玩具互动研究：通过参与式设计探讨儿童如何理解AI玩具的边界、能动性和关系


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI正越来越多地融入儿童的日常生活，不仅通过屏幕，还通过所谓的"无屏幕"AI玩具。这些玩具可以模拟情感、个性化回应并回忆先前的互动，创造出持续社会联系的错觉。这些能力引发了重要问题：儿童在与AI玩具互动时如何理解边界、能动性和关系。

Method: 进行了两次参与式设计会议，涉及8名6-11岁的儿童。他们与三种不同的AI玩具互动，在游戏、实验和反思之间切换。

Result: 儿童以真诚的好奇心接近AI玩具，将其视为社会存在。然而，频繁的互动中断以及明显智能与玩具形态之间的不匹配破坏了游戏期望，导致了对抗性游戏行为。

Conclusion: 提出了启示和设计建议，以更透明、适合发展水平和负责任的方式引导儿童与AI玩具的接触。

Abstract: Generative AI (genAI) is increasingly being integrated into children's everyday lives, not only through screens but also through so-called "screen-free" AI toys. These toys can simulate emotions, personalize responses, and recall prior interactions, creating the illusion of an ongoing social connection. Such capabilities raise important questions about how children understand boundaries, agency, and relationships when interacting with AI toys. To investigate this, we conducted two participatory design sessions with eight children ages 6-11 where they engaged with three different AI toys, shifting between play, experimentation, and reflection. Our findings reveal that children approached AI toys with genuine curiosity, profiling them as social beings. However, frequent interaction breakdowns and mismatches between apparent intelligence and toy-like form disrupted expectations around play and led to adversarial play. We conclude with implications and design provocations to navigate children's encounters with AI toys in more transparent, developmentally appropriate, and responsible ways.

</details>


### [27] [The Paradox of Prioritization in Public Sector Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02641)
*Erina Seh-Young Moon,Matthew Tamura,Shion Guha*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了公共部门采用算法优先级排序工具的缺陷，指出优先级排序机制在资源稀缺时会加剧交叉身份群体间的相对差异，并可能强化不平等感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共部门越来越多地采用算法优先级排序工具来分配稀缺公共资源，现有研究主要关注公平性、准确性和有效性评估，但优先级排序本身的结构设计如何影响工具效果和受影响者体验的研究不足，特别是在真实公共部门条件下。

Method: 研究通过分析优先级排序的基本机制，展示在资源日益稀缺的情况下，这些机制如何在不同交叉身份群体间产生显著的相对差异，并论证优先级排序可能加剧受影响个体的不平等感知。

Result: 研究发现优先级排序方法在公共部门存在缺陷，其底层机制在资源稀缺时会在交叉身份群体间产生显著的相对差异。尽管普遍认为资源优先级排序能带来高效分配结果，但实际上可能强化受影响个体的不平等感知。

Conclusion: 算法工具产生的效率不应与"用更少资源做更多事"的主流论述混为一谈，需要关注现实实施环境中被忽视的资源约束风险。公共部门采用优先级排序方法时需要谨慎考虑其结构性影响。

Abstract: Public sector agencies perform the critical task of implementing the redistributive role of the State by acting as the leading provider of critical public services that many rely on. In recent years, public agencies have been increasingly adopting algorithmic prioritization tools to determine which individuals should be allocated scarce public resources. Prior work on these tools has largely focused on assessing and improving their fairness, accuracy, and validity. However, what remains understudied is how the structural design of prioritization itself shapes both the effectiveness of these tools and the experiences of those subject to them under realistic public sector conditions. In this study, we demonstrate the fallibility of adopting a prioritization approach in the public sector by showing how the underlying mechanisms of prioritization generate significant relative disparities between groups of intersectional identities as resources become increasingly scarce. We argue that despite prevailing arguments that prioritization of resources can lead to efficient allocation outcomes, prioritization can intensify perceptions of inequality for impacted individuals. We contend that efficiencies generated by algorithmic tools should not be conflated with the dominant rhetoric that efficiency necessarily entails "doing more with less" and we highlight the risks of overlooking resource constraints present in real-world implementation contexts.

</details>


### [28] [Engagement Is Not Transfer: A Withdrawal Study of a Consumer Social Robot with Autistic Children at Home](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02642)
*Yibo Meng,Guangrui Fan,Bingyi Liu,Yingfangzhong Sun,Ruiqi Chen,Haipeng Mi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 社交机器人干预对自闭症儿童的影响研究：持续接触减少焦虑但限制社交能力向真实世界的迁移，而机器人撤除反而促进社交动机和共情能力的提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探究自闭症儿童与社交机器人的互动是否能转化为对人类社交能力的改善，验证高参与度是否必然带来社交技能的迁移

Method: 采用8周家庭随机对照试验，40名5-9岁自闭症儿童使用Qrobot社交机器人，分为持续接触组和机器人撤除组，通过定量测量和照顾者访谈评估焦虑、社交动机、情绪推理和共情能力

Result: 持续接触组显著降低焦虑，证实情感效益和可用性；但撤除组在社交动机、情绪理解和共情行为方面改善更大；定性分析显示"移交vs孤立"模式：撤除促进向人类社交的重新定向，而持续接触将互动局限于儿童-机器人二元关系

Conclusion: 高参与度不能保证社交技能的迁移，机器人撤除可能通过促进向人类社交的重新定向而带来更好的社交能力发展，这对自闭症干预中社交机器人的使用策略有重要启示

Abstract: This study examines whether engagement with social robots translates into improved human-directed social abilities in autistic children. We conducted an 8-week home-based randomized controlled trial with 40 children aged 5--9 using a commercial social robot (Qrobot). Families were assigned to either continued robot access or robot withdrawal. Quantitative measures and caregiver interviews assessed anxiety, social motivation, emotion inference, and empathy. Results showed that continued robot access significantly reduced anxiety, confirming strong affective benefits and high usability. However, children in the withdrawal group demonstrated greater improvements in social motivation, emotion understanding, and empathic behaviors toward caregivers and peers. Qualitative findings revealed a "handoff versus siloing" pattern: withdrawal promoted reorientation toward human social interaction, while continued access concentrated engagement within the child--robot dyad and limited transfer to real-world contexts. We interpret these results as evidence that high engagement does not guarantee social transfer.

</details>


### [29] [Beyond the AI Tutor: Social Learning with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02677)
*Harsh Kumar,Zi Kang,Mu,Jonathan Vincentius,Ashton Anderson*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 多智能体LLM配置相比单智能体辅导能提升学习效果：在数学问题解决中，同时与导师和同伴互动获得最高测试准确率；在写作任务中，双智能体配置避免了单智能体导致的创意同质化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI教育工具主要采用一对一辅导模式，但学习科学研究表明多方互动（同伴建模、共同建构、接触多元视角）能带来一对一辅导无法实现的学习效益。研究旨在探索多智能体LLM配置是否能超越单智能体导师提供更好的学习效果。

Method: 采用两个对照实验：1) 收敛性问题解决研究(N=315)，参与者解决SAT级别数学问题，采用2×2设计（有无LLM导师×有无LLM同伴，同伴会犯不同类型错误）；2) 发散性写作研究(N=247)，参与者撰写议论文和创意文章，比较无AI辅助、单LLM（Claude或ChatGPT）、双LLM（Claude+ChatGPT）三种条件。

Result: 在数学问题解决中，同时与导师和同伴互动的参与者获得最高的独立测试准确率。在写作任务中，两种LLM条件都提高了文章质量，但只有双智能体配置避免了单智能体辅助导致的创意层面同质化问题。

Conclusion: 多智能体LLM学习环境能够实现人类社交学习研究中长期记录的协作和观察效益，从一对一AI辅导转向更丰富的智能体配置可以解锁这些优势，为教育AI设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Most AI-based educational tools today adopt a one-on-one tutoring paradigm, pairing a single LLM with a single learner. Yet decades of learning science research suggest that multi-party interaction -- through peer modeling, co-construction, and exposure to diverse perspectives -- can produce learning benefits that dyadic tutoring alone cannot. In this paper, we investigate whether multi-agent LLM configurations can enhance learning outcomes beyond what a single LLM tutor provides. We present two controlled experiments spanning distinct learning contexts. In a convergent problem-solving study ($N=315$), participants tackle SAT-level math problems in a 2$\times$2 design that varies the presence of an LLM tutor and LLM peers, each making different kinds of errors (conceptual vs.\ arithmetic); participants who interacted with both a tutor and peers achieved the highest unassisted test accuracy. In a divergent composition study ($N=247$), participants write argumentative and creative essays with either no AI assistance, a single LLM (Claude or ChatGPT), or both Claude and ChatGPT together; while both LLM conditions improved essay quality, only the two-agent condition avoided the idea-level homogeneity that single-model assistance was found to produce. Together, these studies offer one of the first controlled investigations of multi-agent LLM learning environments, probing whether the move from one-on-one AI tutoring toward richer agent configurations can unlock the collaborative and observational benefits long documented in human social learning research.

</details>


### [30] [AI Disclosure with DAISY](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02760)
*Yoana Ahmetoglu,Marios Constantinides,Anna Cox*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: DAISY是一个基于表单的工具，用于生成AI使用披露声明，通过结构化披露提高报告完整性和透明度，同时不降低作者舒适度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI工具在研究中的使用日益普遍，但AI披露声明仍然罕见且不一致。现有政策指导有限，作者在报告AI使用时面临社会、认知和情感障碍，需要探索结构化披露如何影响作者报告内容和体验。

Method: 开发DAISY（Disclosure of AI-uSe in Your Research）表单工具，基于文献需求分析和共同设计（N=11），并在作者用户研究中进行部署（N=31），比较DAISY支持的披露与非结构化披露的差异。

Result: DAISY支持的披露满足更多完整性标准，在研究过程和写作阶段提供更清晰的AI使用细分。令人惊讶的是，尽管作者担心透明披露AI使用可能带来的负面看法，但使用DAISY并未降低他们对披露声明的舒适度。

Conclusion: 结构化披露工具如DAISY可以改善AI使用报告的完整性和透明度，同时维持作者舒适度。研究讨论了设计启示，并提出了将AI披露作为社会技术实践的研究议程。

Abstract: The use of AI tools in research is becoming routine, alongside growing consensus that such use should be transparently disclosed. However, AI disclosure statements remain rare and inconsistent, with policies offering limited guidance and authors facing social, cognitive, and emotional barriers when reporting AI use. To explore how structured disclosure shapes what authors report and how they experience disclosure, we present DAISY (Disclosure of AI-uSe in Your Research), a form-based tool for generating AI disclosure statements. DAISY was developed from literature-derived requirements and co-design (N =11), and deployed in a user study with authors (N=31). DAISY-supported disclosures met more completeness criteria, offering clearer breakdowns of AI use across research and writing than unsupported disclosures. Surprisingly, despite concerns about how transparently disclosed AI use might be perceived, the use of DAISY did not reduce author comfort with the disclosure statements. We discuss design implications and a research agenda for AI disclosure as a sociotechnical practice.

</details>


### [31] [Disrupting Cognitive Passivity: Rethinking AI-Assisted Data Literacy through Cognitive Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02783)
*Yongsu Ahn,Nam Wook Kim,Benjamin Bach*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出认知对齐框架，通过匹配用户认知需求与AI交互模式来避免认知被动性，促进数据素养发展


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI助手在数据分析中可能导致认知被动性，削弱用户发展数据素养的机会，需要更精细的交互策略

Method: 提出认知对齐框架，将AI交互模式（传输式或审议式）与用户认知需求（接受式或审议式）进行匹配

Result: 认知对齐框架为有效人机交互提供了理论指导，避免认知被动性或认知摩擦

Conclusion: 需要动态自适应策略实现认知对齐，促进数据素养发展，并提出了未来研究方向

Abstract: AI chatbots are increasingly stepping into roles as collaborators or teachers in analyzing, visualizing, and reasoning through data and domain problem. Yet, AI's default assistant mode with its comprehensive and one-off responses may undermine opportunities for practitioners to develop literacy through their own thinking, inducing cognitive passivity. Drawing on evidence from empirical studies and theories, we argue that disrupting cognitive passivity necessitates a nuanced approach: rather than simply making AI promote deliberative thinking, there is a need for more dynamic and adaptive strategy through cognitive alignment -- a framework that characterizes effective human-AI interaction as a function of alignment between users' cognitive demand and AI's interaction mode. In the framework, we provide the mapping between AI's interaction mode (transmissive or deliberative) and users' cognitive demand (receptive or deliberative), otherwise leading to either cognitive passivity or friction. We further discuss implications and offer open questions for future research on data literacy.

</details>


### [32] [UnrealVis: A Testing Laboratory of Optimization Techniques in Unreal Engine for Scientific Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02980)
*Matteo Filosa,Andrea Nardocci,Tiziana Catarci,Marco Angelini*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: UnrealVis是一个基于虚幻引擎的优化实验室，用于在交互式探索中配置和评估渲染技术，帮助用户在性能和保真度之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可视化大型3D科学数据集需要在性能和保真度之间取得平衡，但传统工具通常需要过多的技术专业知识，这限制了非专业用户的有效使用。

Method: 通过综述55篇论文建立了包含22种优化技术的分类法，涵盖六个技术家族，并通过虚幻引擎子系统（如Nanite、LOD方案和剔除技术）实现这些优化。系统提供直观的工作流程，包括实时遥测和A/B比较功能，用于本地和全局性能分析。

Result: 通过核糖体结构和体积流场的案例研究以及专家评估验证，UnrealVis能够帮助用户选择满足性能目标同时保持结构保真度的优化组合。系统已在GitHub上开源。

Conclusion: UnrealVis为大型3D科学数据集的可视化提供了一个有效的优化配置和评估平台，降低了技术门槛，使非专业用户也能在交互式探索中平衡性能和保真度。

Abstract: Visualizing large 3D scientific datasets requires balancing performance and fidelity, but traditional tools often demand excessive technical expertise. We introduce UnrealVis, an Unreal Engine optimization laboratory for configuring and evaluating rendering techniques during interactive exploration. Following a review of 55 papers, we established a taxonomy of 22 optimization techniques across six families, implementing them through engine subsystems such as Nanite, Level of Detail(LOD) schemes, and culling. The system features an intuitive workflow with live telemetry and A/B comparisons for local and global performance analysis. Validated through case studies of ribosomal structures and volumetric flow fields, along with an expert evaluation, UnrealVis facilitates the selection of optimization combinations that meet performance goals while preserving structural fidelity. UnrealVis is available at https://github.com/XAIber-lab/UnrealVis

</details>


### [33] [MECO: A Multimodal Dataset for Emotion and Cognitive Understanding in Older Adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03050)
*Hongbin Chen,Jie Li,Wei Wang,Siyang Song,Xiao Gu,Jianqing Li,Wentao Xiang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: MECO是一个针对老年人的多模态情感与认知数据集，包含42名参与者的视频、音频、EEG和ECG数据，提供情感和认知状态标注，填补了老龄化人群多模态情感计算研究的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态情感计算研究主要针对年轻、认知健康的受试者，缺乏针对老龄化人群的数据集，而认知衰退会影响情感表达和生理反应，因此需要专门的数据集来研究老年人的情感与认知关系。

Method: 在社区环境中采用标准化协议收集数据，包含42名参与者约38小时的多模态信号，涵盖视频、音频、脑电图和心电图，同步生成30,592个样本，并提供情感（效价、唤醒度、六种基本情绪）和认知（MMSE评分）的全面标注。

Result: 建立了MECO数据集，包含多模态信号和综合标注，为情感和认知预测提供了基准测试，可作为老龄化人群多模态建模的基础资源。

Conclusion: MECO填补了老龄化人群多模态情感计算研究的空白，为个性化情感识别和轻度认知障碍早期检测等实际应用提供了基础，数据集已公开可用。

Abstract: While affective computing has advanced considerably, multimodal emotion prediction in aging populations remains underexplored, largely due to the scarcity of dedicated datasets. Existing multimodal benchmarks predominantly target young, cognitively healthy subjects, neglecting the influence of cognitive decline on emotional expression and physiological responses. To bridge this gap, we present MECO, a Multimodal dataset for Emotion and Cognitive understanding in Older adults. MECO includes 42 participants and provides approximately 38 hours of multimodal signals, yielding 30,592 synchronized samples. To maximize ecological validity, data collection followed standardized protocols within community-based settings. The modalities cover video, audio, electroencephalography (EEG), and electrocardiography (ECG). In addition, the dataset offers comprehensive annotations of emotional and cognitive states, including self-assessed valence, arousal, six basic emotions, and Mini-Mental State Examination cognitive scores. We further establish baseline benchmarks for both emotion and cognitive prediction. MECO serves as a foundational resource for multimodal modeling of affect and cognition in aging populations, facilitating downstream applications such as personalized emotion recognition and early detection of mild cognitive impairment (MCI) in real-world settings. The complete dataset and supplementary materials are available at https://maitrechen.github.io/meco-page/.

</details>


### [34] [Same Feedback, Different Source: How AI vs. Human Feedback Attribution and Credibility Shape Learner Behavior in Computing Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03075)
*Caitlin Morris,Pattie Maes*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨学习者对反馈来源的认知是否影响学习效果，通过三组实验比较AI即时反馈、AI延迟反馈和人类延迟反馈，发现来源归因和交付时间对学习结果有不同影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统越来越多地承担教学角色，一个重要问题出现：学习者认为反馈来自谁是否会影响学习效果？先前研究未能控制交付时间这一混淆变量，本研究旨在分离来源归因和交付时间的影响。

Method: 采用三条件实验设计（N=148），参与者完成创意编码教程并接收来自同一大型语言模型生成的反馈。三组分别为：AI系统（即时交付）、AI系统（延迟交付）、人类助教（匹配延迟交付）。这种设计分离了来源归因和交付时间的影响。

Result: 1) 来源归因影响任务时间：认为反馈来自人类的参与者比接收同等时间AI反馈的参与者花费更多时间（d=0.61, p=.013）。2) 交付延迟独立增加输出复杂度但不影响时间。3) 探索性分析显示46%人类归因组参与者不相信该归因，这些参与者比接收透明AI反馈的参与者表现更差（代码复杂度d=0.77, p=.003；任务时间d=0.70, p=.007）。

Conclusion: 学习者认为的人类存在可能具有激励价值，但这种价值取决于可信度。对于计算机教育者，在人类归因不可信的情境中，透明的AI归因可能是风险较低的默认选择。

Abstract: As AI systems increasingly take on instructional roles - providing feedback, guiding practice, evaluating work - a fundamental question emerges: does it matter to learners who they believe is on the other side? We investigated this using a three-condition experiment (N=148) in which participants completed a creative coding tutorial and received feedback generated by the same large language model, attributed to either an AI system (with instant or delayed delivery) or a human teaching assistant (with matched delayed delivery). This three-condition design separates the effect of source attribution from the confound of delivery timing, which prior studies have not controlled. Source attribution and timing had distinct effects on different outcomes: participants who believed the human attribution spent more time on task than those receiving equivalently timed AI-attributed feedback (d=0.61, p=.013, uncorrected), while the delivery delay independently increased output complexity without affecting time measures. An exploratory analysis revealed that 46% of participants in the human-attributed condition did not believe the attribution, and these participants showed worse outcomes than those receiving transparent AI feedback (code complexity d=0.77, p=.003; time on task d=0.70, p=.007). These findings suggest that believed human presence may carry motivational value, but that this value depends on credibility. For computing educators, transparent AI attribution may be the lower-risk default in contexts where human attribution would not be credible.

</details>
