### [35] [OmniTQA: A Cost-Aware System for Hybrid Query Processing over Semi-Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02444)
*Nima Shahbazi,Seiji Maekawa,Nikita Bhutani,Estevam Hruschka*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: OmniTQA是一个成本感知的混合查询处理框架，用于处理同时包含结构化属性和自由文本字段的企业数据库，通过将语义推理作为一等查询操作符，将LLM语义操作与传统关系运算符集成到可执行DAG中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-SQL和表格问答系统通常假设所有查询相关信息都明确表示在结构化模式中，但实际企业数据库常包含结构化属性和自由文本字段共存的混合模式，需要系统能够同时推理这两种类型的信息。

Method: 提出OmniTQA框架：1) 将语义推理作为一等查询操作符，将LLM语义操作与传统关系运算符集成到可执行有向无环图；2) 扩展经典查询优化，采用数据感知规划，结合原子查询分解和操作符重排序以最小化语义工作负载；3) 双引擎执行架构，动态在关系数据库和LLM模块间路由任务，使用操作符感知批处理实现高效扩展。

Result: 在多样化的结构化和半结构化表格问答基准测试中，OmniTQA在准确性和成本效率方面持续优于现有的符号、语义和混合基线方法。这些优势在复杂查询、大型表格和多关系模式中尤为明显。

Conclusion: OmniTQA成功解决了混合模式数据库的查询处理挑战，通过将语义推理作为一等操作符并采用成本感知优化，实现了在准确性和效率方面的显著改进，为实际企业数据库应用提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: While recent advances in large language models have significantly improved Text-to-SQL and table question answering systems, most existing approaches assume that all query-relevant information is explicitly represented in structured schemas. In practice, many enterprise databases contain hybrid schemas where structured attributes coexist with free-form textual fields, requiring systems to reason over both types of information. To address this challenge, we introduce OmniTQA, a cost-aware hybrid query processing framework that operates over both structured and semi-structured data. OmniTQA treats semantic reasoning as a first-class query operator, seamlessly integrating LLM-based semantic operations with classical relational operators into an executable directed acyclic graph. To manage the high latency and cost of LLM inference, it extends classical query optimization with data-aware planning, combining atomic query decomposition and operator reordering to minimize semantic workload. The framework also features a dual-engine execution architecture that dynamically routes tasks between a relational database and an LLM module, using operator-aware batching to scale efficiently. Extensive experiments across a diverse suite of structured and semi-structured table question answering benchmarks demonstrate that OmniTQA consistently outperforms existing symbolic, semantic, and hybrid baselines in both accuracy and cost efficiency. These gains are particularly pronounced for complex queries, large tables and multi-relation schemas.

</details>


### [36] [Efficient Path Query Processing in Relational Database Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02553)
*Diego Rivera Correa,Mirek Riedewald*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: ReCAP：一种在关系型DBMS中实现路径查询早期过滤的抽象框架，通过显式指定过滤机会，性能提升可达40万倍


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前图数据库和关系数据库在处理结合标签正则表达式和属性值约束的通用路径查询时，无法有效进行中间结果的早期过滤，导致查询效率低下

Method: 提出ReCAP抽象框架，只需实现NFA风格的状态转移函数和少量类似用户定义聚合的函数，即可在关系型DBMS中显式指定早期过滤机会，将属性约束深度推入查询计划

Result: 使用ReCAP后，标准关系型DBMS（如DuckDB）能够有效进行属性约束的早期过滤，在各种查询和输入图上，性能比现有最佳图数据库和关系数据库提升高达400,000倍

Conclusion: ReCAP通过简化早期过滤技术的实现，使关系型DBMS能够高效处理通用路径查询，为路径查询优化提供了实用的解决方案

Abstract: Path queries are crucial for property graphs, and there is growing interest in queries that combine regular expressions over labels with constraints on property values of vertices and edges. Efficient evaluation of such general path queries requires that intermediate results be eliminated early when there is no possible completion to a full result path. Neither state-of-the-art (SOA) graph DBMS nor relational DBMS currently can do this effectively for a large class of queries. We show that this problem can be addressed by giving a relational optimizer ``a little help'' by specifying early filtering opportunities explicitly in the query. To this end, we propose ReCAP, an abstraction that greatly simplifies the implementation of early filtering techniques for any type of property constraint for which such early filtering can be derived. No matter how complex the constraint, one only needs to implement (1) an NFA-style state transition function and (2) a handful of functions that mirror those needed for user-defined aggregates. We show that when using ReCAP, a standard relational DBMS like DuckDB can effectively push property constraints deep into the query plan, beating the SOA graph and relational DBMS by a factor up to 400,000 over a variety of queries and input graphs.

</details>


### [37] [Semantic Data Processing with Holistic Data Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02655)
*Youran Sun,Sepanta Zeighami,Bhavya Chopra,Shreya Shankar,Aditya G. Parameswaran*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: HoldUp提出了一种具有整体数据理解能力的语义数据处理新方法，通过联合处理记录并利用跨记录关系来准确解释数据上下文中的任务，解决了现有语义操作符独立处理记录导致准确率低的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义操作符在处理数据时存在关键缺陷：缺乏整体数据理解。这些操作符通常使用LLM独立处理每个数据记录，不考虑数据上下文，仅依赖LLM对用户提供任务的与数据集无关的解释。然而自然语言不精确，任务只有在数据集上下文中正确解释时才能准确执行，导致分类和评分等典型语义映射任务在广泛数据集中产生不准确结果。

Method: HoldUp采用联合处理记录的方法，利用跨记录关系在数据上下文中正确解释任务。为解决LLM数据理解悖论（需要大量代表性数据子集提供上下文，但长输入会导致质量下降），开发了新颖的聚类算法，通过明智使用LLM识别数据集中的潜在结构，受bagging启发。以此为基础，开发了基于聚类的分类和评分方法。

Result: 在15个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，HoldUp始终优于现有解决方案，分类任务准确率提高达33%，评分和聚类任务准确率提高达30%。

Conclusion: HoldUp通过整体数据理解解决了现有语义操作符的关键缺陷，采用联合处理记录和基于聚类的算法，显著提高了语义数据处理任务的准确率，为LLM在数据系统中的有效应用提供了新方法。

Abstract: Semantic operators have increasingly become integrated within data systems to enable processing data using Large Language Models (LLMs). Despite significant recent effort in improving these operators, their accuracy is limited due to a critical flaw in their implementation: lack of holistic data understanding. In existing systems, semantic operators often process each data record independently using an LLM, without considering data context, only leveraging LLM's dataset-agnostic interpretation of the user-provided task. However, natural language is imprecise, so a task can only be accurately performed if it is correctly interpreted in the context of the dataset. For example, for classification and scoring tasks, which are typical semantic map tasks, the standard method of processing each record row by row yields inaccurate results in a wide range of datasets. We propose HoldUp, a new method for semantic data processing with holistic data understanding. HoldUp processes records jointly, leveraging cross-record relationships to correctly interpret the task within the data context. Enabling holistic data understanding, however, is challenging due to what we call LLM data understanding paradox: while large representative data subsets are necessary to provide context, feeding long inputs to LLMs causes quality degradation due to well-known long-context issues. To resolve this paradox, we develop a novel clustering algorithm to identify the latent structure within the dataset through judicious use of LLMs, inspired by bagging. Using this approach as a primitive, we develop novel clustering-based classification and scoring methods to perform these two tasks with high accuracy. Experiments across 15 real-world datasets show that HoldUp consistently outperforms existing solutions, providing up to 33% higher accuracy for classification and 30% higher accuracy for scoring and clustering tasks.

</details>


### [38] [Distance Comparison Operations Are Not Silver Bullets in Vector Similarity Search: A Benchmark Study on Their Merits and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02801)
*Zhuanglin Zheng,Yuxiang Zeng,Chenchen Liu,Yunzhen Chi,Binhan Yang,Yongxin Tong*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 对8种距离比较操作算法在10个数据集和多种硬件配置下的综合基准测试表明，这些方法并非万能解决方案，其效率对数据维度敏感，在分布外查询下性能下降，且硬件间表现不稳定，目前尚不适合生产部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 距离比较操作是向量相似性搜索的关键性能瓶颈，近期避免全维度距离计算的DCO方法承诺显著加速，但其在生产向量数据库系统中的实际适用性仍不明确。

Method: 对8种DCO算法在10个数据集（最多1亿向量，12,288维度）和多样化硬件配置（带/不带SIMD的CPU，GPU）上进行综合基准测试。

Result: DCO方法的效率高度依赖数据维度，在分布外查询下性能下降，硬件间表现不稳定，有时甚至比全维度扫描更慢。但研究也发现其常被忽视的优点：可加速索引构建和数据更新。

Conclusion: 尽管DCO方法具有加速索引构建和数据更新的潜在优势，但其不稳定的性能表现（可能比全维度扫描更慢）表明近期DCO算法进展尚未达到生产部署的成熟度。

Abstract: Distance Comparison Operations (DCOs), which decide whether the distance between a data vector and a query is within a threshold, are a critical performance bottleneck in vector similarity search. Recent DCO methods that avoid full-dimensional distance computations promise significant speedups, but their readiness for production vector database systems remains an open question. To address this, we conduct a comprehensive benchmark of 8 DCO algorithms across 10 datasets (with up to 100M vectors and 12,288 dimensions) and diverse hardware configurations (CPUs with/without SIMD, and GPUs). Our study reveals that these methods are not silver bullets: their efficiency is highly sensitive to data dimensionality, degrades under out-of-distribution queries, and is unstable across hardware. Yet, our evaluation also demonstrates often-overlooked merits: they can accelerate index construction and data updates. Despite these benefits, their unstable performance, which can be slower than a full-dimensional scan, leads us to conclude that recent algorithmic advancements in DCO are not yet ready for production deployment.

</details>


### [39] [Unified and Efficient Approach for Multi-Vector Similarity Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02815)
*Binhan Yang,Yuxiang Zeng,Hengxin Zhang,Zhuanglin Zheng,Yunzhen Chi,Yongxin Tong,Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: MV-HNSW：首个为多向量数据设计的原生层次图索引，通过创新的边权重函数、加速相似度计算算法和增强搜索策略，显著提升检索性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多向量相似性搜索方法基于单向量索引的过滤-精炼框架，将多向量对象中的token向量孤立处理，忽略了它们之间的相关性。这导致一个固有困境：激进的过滤会牺牲召回率，而保守的过滤则在精炼阶段产生过高的计算成本。

Method: 提出MV-HNSW，首个为多向量数据设计的原生层次图索引。包括：1）满足对称性、基数鲁棒性和查询一致性等基本属性的新颖边权重函数；2）加速的多向量相似度计算算法；3）增强的搜索策略，能够动态发现拓扑上不连通但相关的候选对象。

Result: 在七个真实世界数据集上的广泛实验表明，MV-HNSW实现了最先进的搜索性能，保持超过90%的召回率，同时相比现有方法将搜索延迟降低高达14.0倍。

Conclusion: MV-HNSW通过原生支持多向量数据的层次图索引，有效解决了现有过滤-精炼框架的局限性，为细粒度语义检索提供了高效、准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-Vector Similarity Search is essential for fine-grained semantic retrieval in many real-world applications, offering richer representations than traditional single-vector paradigms. Due to the lack of native multi-vector index, existing methods rely on a filter-and-refine framework built upon single-vector indexes. By treating token vectors within each multi-vector object in isolation and ignoring their correlations, these methods face an inherent dilemma: aggressive filtering sacrifices recall, while conservative filtering incurs prohibitive computational cost during refinement. To address this limitation, we propose MV-HNSW, the first native hierarchical graph index designed for multi-vector data. MV-HNSW introduces a novel edge-weight function that satisfies essential properties (symmetry, cardinality robustness, and query consistency) for graph-based indexing, an accelerated multi-vector similarity computation algorithm, and an augmented search strategy that dynamically discovers topologically disconnected yet relevant candidates. Extensive experiments on seven real-world datasets show that MV-HNSW achieves state-of-the-art search performance, maintaining over 90% recall while reducing search latency by up to 14.0$\times$ compared to existing methods.

</details>


### [40] [LLM+Graph@VLDB'2025 Workshop Summary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02861)
*Yixiang Fang,Arijit Khan,Tianxing Wu,Da Yan,Shu Wang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 第二届LLM+图研讨会聚焦大语言模型与图结构数据融合的前沿研究，探讨算法与系统创新


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型与图结构数据的结合已成为快速发展的研究前沿，吸引了学术界和工业界的广泛关注。随着图数据管理和图机器学习技术的成熟，如何有效整合LLMs与图数据成为推动实际应用的关键挑战。

Method: 通过举办第二届LLM+图研讨会，汇集领域专家分享最新研究成果。研讨会作为VLDB 2025的附属会议，聚焦于开发连接LLMs、图数据管理和图机器学习的算法与系统。

Result: 研讨会报告突出了该领域的关键研究方向、挑战和创新解决方案，展示了学术界和工业界在LLM+图融合方面的最新进展。

Conclusion: LLM与图数据的融合是一个充满活力的研究领域，研讨会为促进这一交叉领域的发展提供了重要平台，推动了算法和系统创新，为实际应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: The integration of large language models (LLMs) with graph-structured data has become a pivotal and fast evolving research frontier, drawing strong interest from both academia and industry. The 2nd LLM+Graph Workshop, co-located with the 51st International Conference on Very Large Data Bases (VLDB 2025) in London, focused on advancing algorithms and systems that bridge LLMs, graph data management, and graph machine learning for practical applications. This report highlights the key research directions, challenges, and innovative solutions presented by the workshop's speakers.

</details>

### [41] [A Synthesis Method of Safe Rust Code Based on Pushdown Colored Petri Nets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02399)
*Kaiwen Zhang,Guanjun Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出基于推下有色Petri网(PCPN)的Rust代码合成方法，通过建模所有权、借用和生命周期约束，从公共API签名合成有效调用序列


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rust的安全内存保证依赖于编译时的严格约束（所有权转移、借用规则、生命周期），自动合成正确且安全的Rust代码具有挑战性，因为生成的代码必须同时满足这些约束以及类型和接口要求

Method: 定义推下有色Petri网(PCPN)直接建模编译约束：令牌颜色编码动态资源状态和生命周期区域，推下栈通过压入/弹出令牌跟踪生命周期参数的进入/离开，转移仅在类型匹配、接口义务满足且所需资源状态可用时才被启用

Result: 基于互模拟理论证明PCPN的启用和触发规则与这三种约束的编译时检查一致，开发了基于PCPN的自动合成工具，实验结果显示所有合成代码都是正确的

Conclusion: 提出的PCPN方法能够有效建模Rust的编译约束，为自动合成正确且安全的Rust代码提供了理论基础和实用工具

Abstract: Safe Rust guarantees memory safety through strict compile-time constraints: ownership can be transferred, borrowing can temporarily guarantee either shared read-only or exclusive write access, and ownership and borrowing are scoped by lifetime. Automatically synthesizing correct and safe Rust code is challenging, as the generated code must not only satisfy ownership, borrowing, and lifetime constraints, but also meet type and interface requirements at compile time. This work proposes a synthesis method based on our newly defined Pushdown Colored Petri Net (PCPN) that models these compilation constraints directly from public API signatures to synthesize valid call sequences. Token colors encode dynamic resource states together with a scope level indicating the lifetime region in which a borrow is valid. The pushdown stack tracks the entering or leaving of lifetime parameter via pushing and popping tokens. A transition is enabled only when type matching and interface obligations both hold and the required resource states are available. Based on the bisimulation theory, we prove that the enabling and firing rules of PCPN are consistent with the compile-time check of these three constraints. We develop an automatic synthesis tool based on PCPN and the experimental results show that the synthesized codes are all correct.

</details>


### [42] [TypePro: Boosting LLM-Based Type Inference via Inter-Procedural Slicing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02702)
*Teyu Lin,Minghao Fan,Huaxun Huang,Zhirong Shen,Rongxin Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TypePro：一种利用LLM进行动态语言类型推断的方法，通过过程间代码切片补充上下文信息，并基于切片中的数据类型结构信息提出候选复杂类型，显著提升了类型推断的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态语言（如Python和JavaScript）虽然提供了灵活性和简化的类型处理，但也导致类型相关错误增加和编译时类型推断的额外开销。现有方法仅关注与类型推断直接相关的变量依赖作为上下文，导致上下文信息不完整，影响了类型推断的准确性。

Method: 提出TypePro方法：1）利用LLM进行动态语言类型推断；2）通过过程间代码切片补充上下文信息；3）基于切片中隐含的数据类型结构信息提出候选复杂类型，以解决LLM领域知识不足的问题。

Result: 在ManyTypes4Py和ManyTypes4TypeScript数据集上的实验表明，TypePro分别达到了88.9%和86.6%的Top-1精确匹配率，相比次优方法分别提升了7.1和10.3个百分点，证明了方法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TypePro通过过程间代码切片补充上下文信息和基于结构信息提出候选复杂类型，显著提升了动态语言类型推断的准确性，为解决LLM在类型推断中领域知识不足的问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Dynamic languages (such as Python and JavaScript) offer flexibility and simplified type handling for programming, but this can also lead to an increase in type-related errors and additional overhead for compile-time type inference. As a result, type inference for dynamic languages has become a popular research area. Existing approaches typically achieve type inference through static analysis, machine learning, or large language models (LLMs). However, current work only focuses on the direct dependencies of variables related to type inference as the context, resulting in incomplete contextual information and thus affecting the accuracy of type inference. To address this issue, this paper proposes a method called TypePro, which leverages LLMs for type inference in dynamic languages. TypePro supplements contextual information by conducting inter-procedural code slicing. Then, TypePro proposes a set of candidate complex types based on the structural information of data types implied in the slices, thereby addressing the lack of domain knowledge of LLMs. We conducted experiments on the ManyTypes4Py and ManyTypes4TypeScript datasets, achieving Top-1 exact match (EM) rates of 88.9% and 86.6%, respectively. Notably, TypePro improves the Top-1 Exact Match by 7.1 and 10.3 percentage points over the second-best approach, showing the effectiveness and robustness of TypePro.

</details>


### [43] [What Are Adversaries Doing? Automating Tactics, Techniques, and Procedures Extraction: A Systematic Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02377)
*Mahzabin Tamanna,Shaswata Mitra,Md Erfan,Ahmed Ryan,Sudip Mittal,Laurie Williams,Md Rayhanur Rahman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对80篇同行评审研究的系统分析，总结了从非结构化文本中提取攻击战术、技术和程序（TTP）的研究现状、趋势和局限性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 攻击者不断演进其TTP以达成目标并规避检测，需要防御者持续更新对攻击行为的理解。现有研究在提取目标、数据集、建模方法和评估实践上差异很大，难以理解研究现状

Method: 系统分析80篇同行评审研究，从提取目的、数据源、数据集构建、建模方法、评估指标和成果可用性等关键维度进行分析

Result: 发现技术级分类仍是主要任务形式，战术分类和技术搜索研究不足；领域从基于规则和传统机器学习发展到基于Transformer架构，最近研究探索LLM方法但采用仍处于早期；存在单标签分类、有限评估设置和狭窄数据集等限制，可复现性受专有数据集、有限代码发布和受限语料库影响

Conclusion: 该研究为安全研究人员提供了从非结构化文本中提取TTP的研究现状概览，识别了当前趋势和局限性，为未来研究提供了方向

Abstract: Adversaries continuously evolve their tactics, techniques, and procedures (TTPs) to achieve their objectives while evading detection, requiring defenders to continually update their understanding of adversary behavior. Prior research has proposed automated extraction of TTP-related intelligence from unstructured text and mapping it to structured knowledge bases, such as MITRE ATT&CK. However, existing work varies widely in extraction objectives, datasets, modeling approaches, and evaluation practices, making it difficult to understand the research landscape. The goal of this study is to aid security researchers in understanding the state of the art in extracting attack tactics, techniques, and procedures (TTPs) from unstructured text by analyzing relevant literature. We systematically analyze 80 peer-reviewed studies across key dimensions: extraction purposes, data sources, dataset construction, modeling approaches, evaluation metrics, and artifact availability. Our analysis reveals several dominant trends. Technique-level classification remains the dominant task formulation, while tactic classification and technique searching are underexplored. The field has progressed from rule-based and traditional machine learning to transformer-based architectures (e.g., BERT, SecureBERT, RoBERTa), with recent studies exploring LLM-based approaches including prompting, retrieval-augmented generation, and fine-tuning, though adoption remains emergent. Despite these advances, important limitations persist: many studies rely on single-label classification, limited evaluation settings, and narrow datasets, constraining cross-domain generalization. Reproducibility is further hindered by proprietary datasets, limited code releases, and restricted corpora.

</details>


### [44] [Ambig-IaC: Multi-level Disambiguation for Interactive Cloud Infrastructure-as-Code Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02382)
*Zhenning Yang,Kaden Gruizenga,Tongyuan Miao,Patrick Tser Jern Kon,Hui Guan,Ang Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于分歧驱动的训练免费框架，用于处理IaC配置生成中的模糊性问题，通过生成多样化候选、识别结构分歧、排序信息量并生成针对性澄清问题来逐步缩小配置空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代云基础设施的规模和复杂性使得基础设施即代码(IaC)对部署管理至关重要。虽然大型语言模型(LLMs)越来越多地用于从自然语言生成IaC配置，但用户请求往往不够明确。与传统代码生成不同，IaC配置无法廉价执行或迭代修复，迫使LLMs几乎处于一次性生成模式。观察到IaC中的模糊性具有可处理的组合结构：配置可分解为三个层次轴(资源、拓扑、属性)，其中高层决策约束低层决策。

Method: 提出训练免费的、分歧驱动的框架：1)生成多样化候选规范；2)识别这些轴上的结构分歧；3)按信息量对分歧排序；4)生成针对性澄清问题，逐步缩小配置空间。引入Ambig-IaC基准，包含300个经过验证的具有模糊提示的IaC任务，以及基于图编辑距离和嵌入相似度的评估框架。

Result: 该方法优于最强基线，在结构和属性评估上分别实现了+18.4%和+25.4%的相对改进。

Conclusion: 通过利用IaC模糊性的组合结构，提出的分歧驱动框架能够有效处理模糊用户请求，通过生成针对性澄清问题来逐步缩小配置空间，显著提高了IaC配置生成的准确性和效率。

Abstract: The scale and complexity of modern cloud infrastructure have made Infrastructure-as-Code (IaC) essential for managing deployments. While large Language models (LLMs) are increasingly being used to generate IaC configurations from natural language, user requests are often underspecified. Unlike traditional code generation, IaC configurations cannot be executed cheaply or iteratively repaired, forcing the LLMs into an almost one-shot regime. We observe that ambiguity in IaC exhibits a tractable compositional structure: configurations decompose into three hierarchical axes (resources, topology, attributes) where higher-level decisions constrain lower-level ones. We propose a training-free, disagreement-driven framework that generates diverse candidate specifications, identifies structural disagreements across these axes, ranks them by informativeness, and produces targeted clarification questions that progressively narrow the configuration space. We introduce \textsc{Ambig-IaC}, a benchmark of 300 validated IaC tasks with ambiguous prompts, and an evaluation framework based on graph edit distance and embedding similarity. Our method outperforms the strongest baseline, achieving relative improvements of +18.4\% and +25.4\% on structure and attribute evaluations, respectively.

</details>


### [45] [Improving MPI Error Detection and Repair with Large Language Models and Bug References](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02398)
*Scott Piersall,Yang Gao,Shenyang Liu,Liqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种结合Few-Shot Learning、Chain-of-Thought推理和Retrieval Augmented Generation技术的MPI程序错误检测与修复方法，显著提升了大型语言模型在MPI程序错误检测方面的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPI是高性能计算中的基础技术，广泛用于大规模模拟和分布式训练，但MPI程序的维护具有挑战性。虽然大型语言模型如ChatGPT可用于自动化错误检测和修复，但直接应用效果不佳，因为这些模型缺乏MPI程序正确和错误使用的相关知识。

Method: 设计了一种结合Few-Shot Learning（FSL）、Chain-of-Thought（CoT）推理和Retrieval Augmented Generation（RAG）技术的错误检测与修复方法，增强大型语言模型检测和修复MPI程序错误的能力。

Result: 该方法将错误检测准确率从基线方法（直接使用ChatGPT）的44%提升到77%，显著提高了检测效果。实验还表明该错误引用技术能够很好地泛化到其他大型语言模型。

Conclusion: 通过结合FSL、CoT和RAG技术，可以显著增强大型语言模型在MPI程序错误检测和修复方面的能力，为解决MPI程序维护的挑战提供了有效方法。

Abstract: Message Passing Interface (MPI) is a foundational technology in high-performance computing (HPC), widely used for large-scale simulations and distributed training (e.g., in machine learning frameworks such as PyTorch and TensorFlow). However, maintaining MPI programs remains challenging due to their complex interplay among processes and the intricacies of message passing and synchronization. With the advancement of large language models like ChatGPT, it is tempting to adopt such technology for automated error detection and repair. Yet, our studies reveal that directly applying large language models (LLMs) yields suboptimal results, largely because these models lack essential knowledge about correct and incorrect usage, particularly the bugs found in MPI programs. In this paper, we design a bug detection and repair technique alongside Few-Shot Learning (FSL), Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning, and Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) techniques in LLMs to enhance the large language model's ability to detect and repair errors. Surprisingly, such enhancements lead to a significant improvement, from 44% to 77%, in error detection accuracy compared to baseline methods that use ChatGPT directly. Additionally, our experiments demonstrate our bug referencing technique generalizes well to other large language models.

</details>


### [46] [Runtime Execution Traces Guided Automated Program Repair with Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02647)
*Jiaqing Wu,Tong Wu,Manqing Zhang,Yunwei Dong,Bo Shen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TraceRepair：一种利用运行时事实作为共享约束的多智能体程序修复框架，通过捕获执行快照和委员会交叉验证机制，显著提升复杂逻辑错误的修复能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的自动程序修复方法主要依赖静态源代码和基本测试输出，难以准确捕捉复杂运行时行为和动态数据依赖。虽然引入执行跟踪等运行时证据能暴露具体状态转换，但单一LLM孤立解释这些证据容易过拟合特定假设，产生仅偶然通过测试而非逻辑正确的补丁。因此，需要将运行时证据作为客观约束而非单纯附加输入。

Method: 提出TraceRepair多智能体框架：1）探测智能体捕获关键变量的执行快照，形成客观修复基础；2）由专门化智能体组成的委员会交叉验证候选补丁，暴露不一致性并迭代精炼补丁。运行时事实作为共享约束用于补丁验证。

Result: 在Defects4J基准测试中，TraceRepair正确修复了392个缺陷，显著优于现有基于LLM的方法。大量实验表明其在效率和泛化能力上的提升，特别是在新构建的近期bug数据集上表现出强泛化性，确认性能提升源于动态推理而非记忆。

Conclusion: TraceRepair通过将运行时证据作为客观约束并采用多智能体交叉验证机制，有效解决了传统LLM-based APR在复杂逻辑错误和静默故障修复中的局限性，实现了更准确、可靠的程序修复。

Abstract: Automated Program Repair (APR) struggles with complex logic errors and silent failures. Current LLM-based APR methods are mostly static, relying on source code and basic test outputs, which fail to accurately capture complex runtime behaviors and dynamic data dependencies. While incorporating runtime evidence like execution traces exposes concrete state transitions, a single LLM interpreting this in isolation often overfits to specific hypotheses, producing patches that satisfy tests by coincidence rather than correct logic. Therefore, runtime evidence should act as objective constraints rather than mere additional input. We propose TraceRepair, a multi-agent framework that leverages runtime facts as shared constraints for patch validation. A probe agent captures execution snapshots of critical variables to form an objective repair basis. Meanwhile, a committee of specialized agents cross-verifies candidate patches to expose inconsistencies and iteratively refine them. Evaluated on the Defects4J benchmark, TraceRepair correctly fixes 392 defects, substantially outperforming existing LLM-based approaches. Extensive experiments demonstrate improved efficiency and strong generalization on a newly constructed dataset of recent bugs, confirming that performance gains arise from dynamic reasoning rather than memorization.

</details>


### [47] [GBQA: A Game Benchmark for Evaluating LLMs as Quality Assurance Engineers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02648)
*Shufan Jiang,Chios Chen,Zhiyang Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: GBQA基准测试评估LLMs在游戏开发中自主发现软件bug的能力，包含30个游戏和124个人工验证的bug，实验显示当前最佳模型仅能发现48.39%的bug


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主发现软件bug是现代软件开发中的重大挑战，相比代码生成，动态运行时环境的复杂性使得大型语言模型在bug发现方面面临更大困难。游戏开发作为代表性领域，需要建立合适的基准来评估LLMs的bug检测能力。

Method: 1. 构建GBQA基准：包含30个游戏和124个人工验证的bug，分为三个难度等级；2. 使用多智能体系统可扩展地开发游戏并注入bug，由人类专家确保正确性；3. 提供基线交互式智能体，配备多轮ReAct循环和记忆机制，支持长时程游戏环境探索以进行bug检测；4. 在不同LLMs上进行广泛实验。

Result: 实验结果表明自主bug发现仍然极具挑战性：表现最佳的模型Claude-4.6-Opus在思考模式下仅能识别48.39%的已验证bug。这表明当前LLMs在自主软件工程方面仍有显著差距。

Conclusion: GBQA为评估LLMs自主发现软件bug的能力提供了适当的测试平台和评估标准。该基准测试显示自主bug发现仍然是一个高度挑战性的任务，进一步的研究进展将有助于缩小自主软件工程领域的差距。

Abstract: The autonomous discovery of bugs remains a significant challenge in modern software development. Compared to code generation, the complexity of dynamic runtime environments makes bug discovery considerably harder for large language models (LLMs). In this paper, we take game development as a representative domain and introduce the Game Benchmark for Quality Assurance (GBQA), a benchmark containing 30 games and 124 human-verified bugs across three difficulty levels, to evaluate whether LLMs can autonomously detect software bugs. The benchmark is constructed using a multi-agent system that develops games and injects bugs in a scalable manner, with human experts in the loop to ensure correctness. Moreover, we provide a baseline interactive agent equipped with a multi-round ReAct loop and a memory mechanism, enabling long-horizon exploration of game environments for bug detection across different LLMs. Extensive experiments on frontier LLMs demonstrate that autonomous bug discovery remains highly challenging: the best-performing model, Claude-4.6-Opus in thinking mode, identifies only 48.39% of the verified bugs. We believe GBQA provides an adequate testbed and evaluation criterion, and that further progress on it will help close the gap in autonomous software engineering.

</details>


### [48] [AgentSZZ: Teaching the LLM Agent to Play Detective with Bug-Inducing Commits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02665)
*Yunbo Lyu,Jieke Shi,Hong Jin Kang,Ratnadira Widyasari,Junda He,Yuqing Niu,Chengran Yang,Junkai Chen,Zhou Yang,Julia Lawall,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AgentSZZ是一个基于LLM智能体的框架，用于识别bug引入提交，通过集成领域知识、任务特定工具和ReAct式循环，在跨文件和ghost提交等挑战性场景中显著优于现有SZZ算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SZZ算法依赖git blame进行行级追踪，无法处理跨文件和ghost提交等常见场景，导致约四分之一bug引入提交无法追踪。此外，固定流程限制了迭代推理和探索能力，而开发者通常通过交互式多工具过程调查bug。

Method: 提出AgentSZZ框架，利用LLM驱动的智能体探索代码库并识别bug引入提交。框架集成任务特定工具、领域知识和ReAct式循环，实现自适应因果追踪。结构化压缩模块通过减少冗余上下文同时保留关键证据来提高效率。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的数据集上，AgentSZZ在所有设置中一致优于最先进的SZZ算法，相比先前LLM方法F1分数提升高达27.2%。在跨文件和ghost提交等挑战性场景中，召回率分别提升高达300%和60%。消融研究表明任务特定工具和领域知识至关重要，压缩工具输出可减少30%以上token消耗且影响可忽略。

Conclusion: AgentSZZ通过智能体框架解决了传统SZZ算法的关键限制，在bug引入提交识别任务上实现了显著性能提升，特别是在传统方法难以处理的跨文件和ghost提交场景中表现突出。

Abstract: The SZZ algorithm is the dominant technique for identifying bug-inducing commits and underpins many software engineering tasks, such as defect prediction and vulnerability analysis. Despite numerous variants, including recent LLM-based approaches, performance remains limited on developer-annotated datasets (e.g., recall of 0.552 on the Linux kernel). A key limitation is the reliance on git blame, which traces line-level changes within the same file, failing in common scenarios such as ghost and cross-file cases-making nearly one-quarter of bug-inducing commits inherently untraceable. Moreover, current approaches follow fixed pipelines that restrict iterative reasoning and exploration, unlike developers who investigate bugs through an interactive, multi-tool process. To address these challenges, we propose AgentSZZ, an agent-based framework that leverages LLM-driven agents to explore repositories and identify bug-inducing commits. Unlike prior methods, AgentSZZ integrates task-specific tools, domain knowledge, and a ReAct-style loop to enable adaptive and causal tracing of bugs. A structured compression module further improves efficiency by reducing redundant context while preserving key evidence. Extensive experiments on three widely used datasets show that AgentSZZ consistently outperforms state-of-the-art SZZ algorithms across all settings, achieving F1-score gains of up to 27.2% over prior LLM-based approaches. The improvements are especially pronounced in challenging scenarios such as cross-file and ghost commits, with recall gains of up to 300% and 60%, respectively. Ablation studies show that task-specific tools and domain knowledge are critical, while compression tool outputs reduce token consumption by over 30% with negligible impact. The replication package is available.

</details>


### [49] [IndustryCode: A Benchmark for Industry Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02729)
*Puyu Zeng,Zhaoxi Wang,Zhixu Duan,Liang Feng,Shaobo Wang,Cunxiang Wang,Jinghang Wang,Bing Zhao,Hu Wei,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: IndustryCode是首个跨多个工业领域和编程语言的综合代码生成基准测试，包含579个子问题，覆盖金融、自动化、航空航天等领域，使用MATLAB、Python、C++、Stata等多种语言，旨在评估LLM在真实工业场景中的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成基准测试主要局限于单一领域和语言，无法有效评估LLM在真实工业应用中的泛化能力，也不能反映复杂工业场景所需的编程熟练度。工业应用需要跨领域、多语言的代码生成能力，而现有基准无法满足这一需求。

Method: 创建IndustryCode基准测试，包含579个子问题，源自125个主要工业挑战，每个问题都有严格的问题描述和测试用例。覆盖金融、自动化、航空航天、遥感等多个工业领域，支持MATLAB、Python、C++、Stata等多种编程语言。

Result: 评估显示，表现最佳的模型Claude 4.5 Opus在子问题上的总体准确率为68.1%，在主问题上的准确率为42.5%。这表明当前LLM在复杂工业代码生成任务中仍有较大提升空间。

Conclusion: IndustryCode填补了现有代码生成基准测试的空白，为评估LLM在真实工业场景中的泛化能力提供了首个跨领域、多语言的综合测试平台。基准数据集和自动化评估代码将在论文接受后公开。

Abstract: Code generation and comprehension by Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as core drivers of industrial intelligence and decision optimization, finding widespread application in fields such as finance, automation, and aerospace. Although recent advancements have demonstrated the remarkable potential of LLMs in general code generation, existing benchmarks are mainly confined to single domains and languages. Consequently, they fail to effectively evaluate the generalization capabilities required for real-world industrial applications or to reflect the coding proficiency demanded by complex industrial scenarios. To bridge this gap, we introduce IndustryCode, the first comprehensive benchmark designed to span multiple industrial domains and programming languages. IndustryCode comprises 579 sub-problems derived from 125 primary industrial challenges, accompanied by rigorous problem descriptions and test cases. It covers a wide range of fields, including finance, automation, aerospace, and remote sensing-and incorporates diverse programming languages such as MATLAB, Python, C++, and Stata. In our evaluation, the top-performing model, Claude 4.5 Opus, achieved an overall accuracy of 68.1% on sub-problems and 42.5% main problems. The benchmark dataset and automated evaluation code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [50] [Sustainability Analysis of Prompt Strategies for SLM-based Automated Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02761)
*Pragati Kumari,Novarun Deb*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了小型语言模型（SLMs）在自动化测试生成中提示工程策略的可持续性影响，发现提示策略对可持续性结果有显著独立影响，甚至超过模型选择的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于提示的自动化测试日益普及，其计算和环境可持续性问题变得重要。现有AI测试可持续性研究主要关注大语言模型，而提示工程策略的影响（尤其是在小型语言模型背景下）尚未得到充分探索。提示设计直接影响推理行为、执行成本和资源利用，即使模型大小固定，这一研究空白亟待填补。

Method: 在受控实验设置下，分析了三种开源小型语言模型上的七种提示策略。评估综合考虑执行时间、令牌使用量、能耗、碳排放和覆盖测试质量，后者通过生成的测试脚本的覆盖分析进行评估。

Result: 提示策略对可持续性结果有显著且独立的影响，通常超过模型选择的影响。推理密集型策略（如思维链和自洽性）获得更高覆盖率，但显著增加执行时间、能耗和碳排放。相比之下，简单策略（如零样本和ReAct）以明显更低的环境成本提供有竞争力的覆盖测试质量，而最少到最多和程序思维策略提供平衡的权衡。

Conclusion: 提示工程策略在小型语言模型驱动的自动化测试生成中具有重要的可持续性影响。研究结果表明，通过选择合适的提示策略，可以在保持测试质量的同时显著降低环境成本，为可持续AI测试实践提供了重要指导。

Abstract: The growing adoption of prompt-based automation in software testing raises important issues regarding its computational and environmental sustainability. Existing sustainability studies in AI-driven testing primarily focus on large language models, leaving the impact of prompt engineering strategies largely unexplored - particularly in the context of Small Language Models (SLMs). This gap is critical, as prompt design directly influences inference behavior, execution cost, and resource utilization, even when model size is fixed. To the best of our knowledge, this paper presents the first systematic sustainability evaluation of prompt engineering strategies for automated test generation using SLMs. We analyze seven prompt strategies across three open-source SLMs under a controlled experimental setup. Our evaluation jointly considers execution time, token usage, energy consumption, carbon emissions, and coverage test quality, the latter assessed through coverage analysis of the generated test scripts. The results show that prompt strategies have a substantial and independent impact on sustainability outcomes, often outweighing the effect of model choice. Reasoning intensive strategies such as Chain of Thought and Self-Consistency achieve higher coverage but incur significantly higher execution time, energy consumption, and carbon emissions. In contrast, simpler strategies such as Zero-Shot and ReAct deliver competitive coverage test quality with markedly lower environmental cost, while Least-to-Most and Program of Thought offer balanced trade-offs.

</details>


### [51] [Evaluating the Environmental Impact of using SLMs and Prompt Engineering for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02776)
*Md Afif Al Mamun,Sayan Nath,Gias Uddin,Novarun Deb*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究首次系统评估了不同提示工程策略对小型语言模型代码生成的准确性和可持续性影响，发现可持续性与准确性往往解耦，链式思维提示在推理能力和能源效率间提供了近乎最优的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型从云端部署转向本地部署的开源小型语言模型，AI辅助编程得以普及，但AI的环境足迹也随之分散。虽然提示工程策略（如链式思维和ReAct）作为外部机制可以优化代码生成而无需修改模型参数，但它们对能耗和碳排放的影响对开发者来说基本不可见。

Method: 研究评估了六种主流提示策略在11个开源模型（参数范围1B到34B）上的表现，使用HumanEval+和MBPP+基准测试。通过测量Pass@1准确率、能耗（kWh）、碳排放（kgCO2eq）和推理延迟，系统分析了提示工程对代码生成准确性和可持续性的影响。

Result: 研究发现可持续性与准确性往往解耦，允许在不牺牲性能的情况下进行显著的环境优化。链式思维作为一种较简单的提示技术，在推理能力和能源效率间提供了近乎最优的平衡。而多重采样策略通常带来不成比例的成本增加。电网碳强度被确定为部署时排放的主导因素。

Conclusion: 该工作为"绿色"提示工程提供了量化基础，使开发者能够在实现高性能代码生成的同时兼顾生态责任。研究强调从业者需要考虑区域能源配置，并指出链式思维提示是实现准确性与可持续性平衡的有效策略。

Abstract: The shift from cloud-hosted Large Language Models (LLMs) to locally deployed open-source Small Language Models (SLMs) has democratized AI-assisted coding; however, it has also decentralized the environmental footprint of AI. While prompting strategies - such as Chain-of-Thought and ReAct - serve as external mechanisms for optimizing code generation without modifying model parameters, their impact on energy consumption and carbon emissions remains largely invisible to developers. This paper presents the first systematic empirical study investigating how different prompt engineering strategies in SLM-based code generation impact code generation accuracy alongside sustainability factors. We evaluate six prominent prompting strategies across 11 open-source models (ranging from 1B to 34B parameters) using the HumanEval+ and MBPP+ benchmarks. By measuring Pass@1 accuracy alongside energy (kWh), carbon emissions (kgCO2eq), and inference latency, we reveal that sustainability often decouples from accuracy, allowing significant environmental optimizations without sacrificing performance. Our findings indicate that Chain-of-Thought, being a simpler prompting technique, can provide a near-optimal balance between reasoning capability and energy efficiency. Conversely, multi-sampling strategies often incur disproportionate costs for marginal gains. Finally, we identify grid carbon intensity as the dominant factor in deployment-time emissions, highlighting the need for practitioners to consider regional energy profiles. This work provides a quantitative foundation for "green" prompt engineering, enabling developers to align high-performance code generation with ecological responsibility.

</details>


### [52] [Dependency-Guided Repository-Level C-to-Rust Translation with Reinforcement Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02852)
*Jia Feng,Wenjie Gan,Cuiyun Gao,Chaozheng Wang,Feng Luo,Xin Xia,Ge Li,Kui Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DepTrans：结合强化学习与依赖引导迭代优化的C到Rust迁移框架，显著提升编译成功率和计算准确性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化C到Rust迁移对提升软件安全性至关重要，但现有方法面临三大挑战：跨文件依赖处理不足、复杂依赖验证困难、缺乏大规模并行数据。传统规则方法生成代码生硬，LLM方法依赖建模不准确。

Method: 提出DepTrans框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 强化对齐语法训练：通过多任务微调和反馈驱动的强化学习提升生成质量；2) 依赖引导迭代优化：捕获细粒度跨文件依赖并迭代优化生成的Rust代码。构建了85k训练样本数据集和145个仓库级实例基准。

Result: DepTrans达到60.7%编译成功率和43.5%计算准确率，分别比最强基线提升22.8和17.3个百分点。成功构建7/15个工业C项目，展示了实际应用潜力。

Conclusion: DepTrans通过结合模型能力增强与结构化推理，有效解决了C到Rust迁移中的关键挑战，在编译成功率和功能准确性方面显著优于现有方法，具备工业应用可行性。

Abstract: Automating C-to-Rust migration is critical for improving software security without sacrificing performance. Traditional rule-based methods struggle with diverse C idioms, often producing rigid and unidiomatic Rust code. Large Language Models (LLMs), trained on massive code corpora, offer a promising alternative by leveraging cross-language generalization to generate more idiomatic and maintainable Rust code. However, several challenges remain. First, existing LLM-based approaches fail to handle cross-file dependencies effectively, either ignoring them or including entire files as context, which limits accurate dependency modeling. Second, complex dependencies and structured inputs and outputs make it difficult to verify syntactic correctness and functional equivalence at the repository level. Third, the lack of large-scale C-Rust parallel data constrains model performance.
  We propose DepTrans, a framework that combines model capability enhancement with structured inference. DepTrans introduces Reinforcement-Aligned Syntax Training to improve generation quality through multi-task fine-tuning and feedback-driven reinforcement learning. It further applies Dependency-Guided Iterative Refinement to capture fine-grained cross-file dependencies and iteratively refine generated Rust code. We construct a dataset of 85k training samples and a benchmark of 145 repository-level instances. Experiments show that DepTrans achieves a 60.7 percent compilation success rate and 43.5 percent computational accuracy, outperforming the strongest baseline by 22.8 and 17.3 percentage points. It also successfully builds 7 of 15 industrial C projects, demonstrating its practical potential.

</details>


### [53] [BugForge: Constructing and Utilizing DBMS Bug Repository to Enhance DBMS Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03024)
*Dawei Li,Qifan Liu,Yuxiao Guo,Jie Liang,Zhiyong Wu,Chi Zhang,Jingzhou Fu,Haogang Mao,Zhenyu Guan,Yu Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: BugForge框架通过构建标准化的DBMS bug仓库，并利用其生成高质量测试用例来增强DBMS测试，在四个主流数据库系统中发现了35个未知bug。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DBMS是复杂系统，容易产生可能导致系统故障或数据完整性受损的bug。建立统一的bug仓库对于系统化组织bug相关数据、改进代码和支持自动化测试至关重要。然而，bug报告的异构性、不完整或不准确内容使得构建统一仓库并将其转化为高质量测试用例具有挑战性。

Method: BugForge框架采用渐进式收集bug报告，然后使用语法感知处理和输入自适应原始PoC提取来构建DBMS bug仓库。仓库存储结构化的bug相关数据，包括bug元数据和包含潜在bug触发语义的原始PoC。这些数据通过语义引导的适应进一步细化为高质量测试用例，从而支持增强的DBMS测试方法，包括DBMS模糊测试、回归测试和跨DBMS bug发现。

Result: BugForge在PostgreSQL、MySQL、MariaDB和MonetDB四个数据库系统中实现，共集成了37,632个bug报告，时间跨度达28年。基于该仓库，BugForge发现了35个先前未知的bug，其中22个已得到开发者确认。

Conclusion: BugForge通过构建和利用bug仓库为DBMS测试提供了有效框架，证明了构建标准化bug仓库并将其转化为高质量测试用例的价值，能够显著增强DBMS测试能力并发现新的bug。

Abstract: DBMSs are complex systems prone to bugs that may lead to system failures or compromise data integrity. Establishing unified DBMS bug repositories is crucial for systematically organizing bug-related data, enabling code improvement, and supporting automated testing. In particular, bug reports often contain valuable test inputs and bug-triggering clues that help explore rare execution paths and expose critical buggy behavior, thereby guiding automated DBMS testing. However, the heterogeneity of bug reports, along with their incomplete or inaccurate content, makes it challenging to build unified repositories and convert them into high-quality test cases.
  In this paper, we propose BugForge, a framework that constructs standardized DBMS bug repositories and leverages them to generate high-quality test cases to enhance DBMS testing. Specifically, BugForge progressively collects bug reports, then employs syntax-aware processing and input-adaptive raw PoC extraction to construct a DBMS bug repository. The repository stores structured bug-related data, including bug metadata and raw PoCs that entail potential bug-triggering semantics. These data are further refined into high-quality test cases through semantic-guided adaptation, thereby enabling enhanced DBMS testing methods, including DBMS fuzzing, regression testing, and cross-DBMS bug discovery. We implemented BugForge for PostgreSQL, MySQL, MariaDB, and MonetDB, totally integrated 37,632 bug reports spanning up to 28 years. Based on the repository, BugForge uncovered 35 previously unknown bugs with 22 confirmed by developers, demonstrating the value of constructing and utilizing bug repositories for DBMS testing.

</details>


### [54] [Combining Static Code Analysis and Large Language Models Improves Correctness and Performance of Algorithm Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03048)
*Denis Neumüller,Sebastian Boll,David Schüler,Matthias Tichy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 结合LLMs与静态分析可显著提升算法识别性能，减少72.39-97.50%的LLM调用，F1分数提升高达12个百分点，且LLM不完全依赖标识符名称信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者花费大量时间理解源代码，自动识别算法能支持程序理解、促进维护并提升软件质量。需要评估LLMs与静态分析结合对算法识别的影响。

Method: 通过多组实验评估LLMs与静态分析结合使用不同过滤模式的效果。对比独立性能与不同提示策略，并研究系统标识符混淆对分类性能和运行时间的影响。

Result: LLMs与轻量级静态分析结合表现优异：LLM调用减少72.39-97.50%，运行时间显著降低，F1分数比基线提升高达12个百分点。上下文学习（两个示例）在分类性能与运行效率间取得最佳平衡（F1 75-77%）。LLMs不完全依赖名称信息，即使标识符混淆仍能识别大多数算法实现。

Conclusion: LLMs与静态分析结合能大幅减少运行时间同时提升F1分数，证明了混合方法的实用价值。

Abstract: Context: Since it is well-established that developers spend a substantial portion of their time understanding source code, the ability to automatically identify algorithms within source code presents a valuable opportunity. This capability can support program comprehension, facilitate maintenance, and enhance overall software quality. Objective: We empirically evaluate how combining LLMs with static code analysis can improve the automated recognition of algorithms, while also evaluating their standalone performance and dependence on identifier names. Method: We perform multiple experiments evaluating the combination of LLMs with static analysis using different filter patterns. We compare this combined approach against their standalone performance under various prompting strategies and investigate the impact of systematic identifier obfuscation on classification performance and runtime. Results: The combination of LLMs with lightweight static analysis performs surprisingly well, reducing required LLM calls by 72.39-97.50% depending on the filter pattern. This not only lowers runtime significantly but also improves F1-scores by up to 12 percentage points (pp) compared to the baseline. Regarding the different prompting strategies, in-context learning with two examples provides an effective trade-off between classification performance and runtime efficiency, achieving F1-scores of 75-77% with only a modest increase in inference time. Lastly, we find that LLMs are not solely dependent on name-information as they are still able to identify most algorithm implementations when identifiers are obfuscated. Conclusion: By combining LLMs with static analysis, we achieve substantial reductions in runtime while simultaneously improving F1-scores, underscoring the value of a hybrid approach.

</details>


### [55] [PAFT: Preservation Aware Fine-Tuning for Minimal-Edit Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03113)
*Boyang Yang,Zijian Cai,Shunfu Jin,Haoye Tain*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PAFT是一种用于最小化编辑的程序修复方法，通过保护感知的微调技术，在保持代码稳定性的同时集中编辑错误区域，显著提高修复成功率并减少不必要的代码修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在程序修复中经常过度编辑代码，即使生成的补丁能通过测试套件，也会重写不必要的代码部分，增加了代码审查和维护成本。传统监督微调缺乏明确的标记级保护信号来指示哪些代码应该保留、哪些应该修改。

Method: PAFT通过对齐错误代码和修复代码来获取标记级保护信号，结合全序列掩码技术，并应用编辑难度课程学习。该方法明确区分应该保留和修改的标记，使模型能够专注于错误区域进行最小化编辑。

Result: 在Defects4J和HumanEval-Java数据集上，PAFT相比标准监督微调将pass@1提高了65.6%，同时将平均编辑距离降低了32.6%。在DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B模型上，PAFT超越了AdaPatcher基线，将pass@1从5.9%提升到10.1%，中位数编辑距离从61.0降低到42.0。

Conclusion: PAFT能够有效保护稳定代码上下文，将编辑集中在错误区域，生成更小、更局部化的合理补丁，且无需推理时搜索、重排或后处理，显著提升了程序修复的效率和代码质量。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are effective for automated program repair, but plausible patches that pass the full test suite often rewrite more code than necessary, increasing review and maintenance costs. This over-editing is common because most bugs are localized, while standard supervised fine-tuning provides no explicit signal about which tokens should be preserved and which should be changed. We propose PAFT, a preservation-aware fine-tuning method for minimal-edit program repair. PAFT derives token-level preservation signals by aligning buggy and fixed code, combines them with full-sequence masking, and applies an edit-difficulty curriculum. Across Defects4J and HumanEval-Java, PAFT improves pass@1 by up to 65.6% over standard supervised fine-tuning (StdFT) while reducing average edit distance (AED) by up to 32.6%. On Defects4J with DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B, PAFT also outperforms AdaPatcher, a strong preference-based repair baseline, improving pass@1 from 5.9% to 10.1% while reducing median AED from 61.0 to 42.0. Overall, PAFT preserves stable context and concentrates edits on faulty regions, yielding smaller, more localized, plausible patches without inference-time search, reranking, or post-processing.

</details>


### [56] [AI-Assisted Unit Test Writing and Test-Driven Code Refactoring: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03135)
*Ema Smolic,Mario Brcic,Luka Hobor,Mihael Kovac*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出使用编码模型进行自动化单元测试生成和后续安全重构的案例研究，通过测试验证代码变更，显著提升测试效率和重构安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多软件系统最初作为原型或最小可行产品开发，注重交付速度而非长期代码可维护性，导致代码库难以修改。在AI辅助编程时代，这种代码质量低下带来了显著的机会成本。

Method: 采用编码模型进行自动化单元测试生成和后续安全重构的案例研究方法。首先迭代生成测试以捕获现有系统行为，然后在开发者监督下进行模型辅助重构，通过测试验证代码变更。

Result: 在数小时内生成了近16,000行可靠的单元测试（而非数周），在关键模块中达到78%的分支覆盖率，显著降低大规模重构期间的回归风险。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了软件工程向经验科学的转变，强调数据收集和约束机制，支持快速、安全的迭代开发。研究还讨论了模型中的错误、局限性以及如何解决观察到的弱价值不对齐问题。

Abstract: Many software systems originate as prototypes or minimum viable products (MVPs), developed with an emphasis on delivery speed and responsiveness to changing requirements rather than long-term code maintainability. While effective for rapid delivery, this approach can result in codebases that are difficult to modify, presenting a significant opportunity cost in the era of AI-assisted or even AI-led programming. In this paper, we present a case study of using coding models for automated unit test generation and subsequent safe refactoring, with proposed code changes validated by passing tests. The study examines best practices for iteratively generating tests to capture existing system behavior, followed by model-assisted refactoring under developer supervision. We describe how this workflow constrained refactoring changes, the errors and limitations observed in both phases, the efficiency gains achieved, when manual intervention was necessary, and how we addressed the weak value misalignment we observed in models. Using this approach, we generated nearly 16,000 lines of reliable unit tests in hours rather than weeks, achieved up to 78\% branch coverage in critical modules, and significantly reduced regression risk during large-scale refactoring. These results illustrate software engineering's shift toward an empirical science, emphasizing data collection and constraining mechanisms that support fast, safe iteration.

</details>

### [57] [Koopman-Based Nonlinear Identification and Adaptive Control of a Turbofan Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01730)
*David Grasev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发了基于Koopman算子的双转子涡扇发动机多变量控制方法，包括自适应Koopman模型预测控制器和反馈线性化控制器，在不同飞行条件下验证了控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Koopman算子方法在涡扇发动机多变量控制中的应用，解决传统控制方法在复杂非线性系统控制中的局限性，提高发动机在不同飞行条件下的控制鲁棒性和性能。

Method: 1. 建立基于物理的部件级模型生成训练数据；2. 开发元启发式扩展动态模态分解方法，设计成本函数准确捕捉转子转速和发动机压力比动态特性；3. 构建时变Koopman模型；4. 开发两种控制器：带扰动观测器的自适应Koopman模型预测控制器和Koopman反馈线性化控制器；5. 在海平面和变化飞行条件下评估两种控制策略。

Result: 1. 提出的辨识方法能准确预测转子转速和发动机压力比；2. Koopman模型可在不同控制结构中灵活重用；3. 两种控制策略在稳态条件下性能相当；4. 自适应Koopman模型预测控制器在变化飞行条件下比反馈线性化控制器具有更好的鲁棒性；5. 发动机压力比控制策略改善了推力响应。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了Koopman基控制在涡扇发动机控制中的适用性，展示了自适应Koopman模型预测控制器框架在提高发动机控制鲁棒性方面的优势，为复杂非线性系统的控制提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates Koopman operator-based approaches for multivariable control of a two-spool turbofan engine. A physics-based component-level model is developed to generate training data and validate the controllers. A meta-heuristic extended dynamic mode decomposition is developed, with a cost function designed to accurately capture both spool-speed dynamics and the engine pressure ratio (EPR), enabling the construction of a single Koopman model suitable for multiple control objectives. Using the identified time-varying Koopman model, two controllers are developed: an adaptive Koopman-based model predictive controller (AKMPC) with a disturbance observer and a Koopman-based feedback linearization controller (K-FBLC), which serves as a benchmark. The controllers are evaluated for two control strategies, namely configurations of spool speeds and EPR, under both sea-level and varying flight conditions. The results demonstrate that the proposed identification approach enables accurate predictions of both spool speeds and EPR, allowing the Koopman model to be reused flexibly across different control formulations. While both control strategies achieve comparable performance in steady conditions, the AKMPC exhibits superior robustness compared with the K-FBLC under varying flight conditions due to its ability to compensate for model mismatch. Moreover, the EPR control strategy improves the thrust response. The study highlights the applicability of Koopman-based control and demonstrates the advantages of the AKMPC-based framework for robust turbofan engine control.

</details>


### [58] [Generating Counterfactual Patient Timelines from Real-World Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02337)
*Yu Akagi,Tomohisa Seki,Toru Takiguchi,Hiromasa Ito,Yoshimasa Kawazoe,Kazuhiko Ohe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于30万患者、4亿条时间线数据的自回归生成模型能够生成临床合理的反事实轨迹，可用于模拟不同临床场景下的患者结局


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实模拟在个性化医疗和计算机临床试验中具有重要应用价值，但受限于方法学挑战。本研究旨在开发能够生成临床合理反事实轨迹的模型。

Method: 使用自回归生成模型，基于30多万患者和4亿条患者时间线条目的真实世界数据进行自监督训练。模型应用于2023年COVID-19住院患者，通过修改年龄、血清C反应蛋白和血清肌酐来模拟7天结局。

Result: 反事实模拟显示：年龄增加、CRP升高和血清肌酐升高均导致院内死亡率上升；CRP值较高时瑞德西韦处方增加，肾功能受损时处方减少。这些反事实轨迹再现了已知的临床模式。

Conclusion: 基于真实世界数据自监督训练的自回归生成模型能够为反事实临床模拟奠定基础，具有转化应用潜力。

Abstract: Counterfactual simulation - exploring hypothetical consequences under alternative clinical scenarios - holds promise for transformative applications such as personalized medicine and in silico trials. However, it remains challenging due to methodological limitations. Here, we show that an autoregressive generative model trained on real-world data from over 300,000 patients and 400 million patient timeline entries can generate clinically plausible counterfactual trajectories. As a validation task, we applied the model to patients hospitalized with COVID-19 in 2023, modifying age, serum C-reactive protein (CRP), and serum creatinine to simulate 7-day outcomes. Increased in-hospital mortality was observed in counterfactual simulations with older age, elevated CRP, and elevated serum creatinine. Remdesivir prescriptions increased in simulations with higher CRP values and decreased in those with impaired kidney function. These counterfactual trajectories reproduced known clinical patterns. These findings suggest that autoregressive generative models trained on real-world data in a self-supervised manner can establish a foundation for counterfactual clinical simulation.

</details>


### [59] [LiME: Lightweight Mixture of Experts for Efficient Multimodal Multi-task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02338)
*Md Kowsher,Haris Mansoor,Nusrat Jahan Prottasha,Ozlem Garibay,Victor Zhu,Zhengping Ji,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LiME提出了一种轻量级混合专家方法，通过单一共享PEFT模块和轻量级专家向量调制实现专家专业化，无需为每个专家单独训练适配器，显著减少了可训练参数并适用于任何PEFT方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE-PEFT方法需要为每个专家单独训练适配器，导致可训练参数随专家数量线性增长，且仅限于基于适配器的架构。这限制了方法的适用性和效率。

Method: LiME采用单一共享PEFT模块，通过轻量级专家向量调制其输出实现专家专业化。引入零参数路由机制，利用现有冻结和适应表示消除每层学习路由参数的需求。还包含n-gram窗口路由和基于路由置信度的自适应专家选择（Auto Top-K）。

Result: 在包含47个跨文本、图像和视频任务的MMT-47多模态多任务基准测试中，LiME实现了竞争性或更优的性能，同时使用比相应MoE-PEFT基线少4倍的可训练参数，训练速度提升达29%。

Conclusion: LiME通过轻量级调制和零参数路由机制，在保持多任务适应性能的同时显著减少了参数开销和训练时间，为混合专家与参数高效微调的结合提供了更通用和高效的解决方案。

Abstract: MoE-PEFT methods combine Mixture of Experts with parameter-efficient fine-tuning for multi-task adaptation, but require separate adapters per expert causing trainable parameters to scale linearly with expert count and limiting applicability to adapter-based architectures. We propose LiME (Lightweight Mixture of Experts), which achieves expert specialization through lightweight modulation rather than adapter replication. Instead of separate adapters, LiME uses a single shared PEFT module and modulates its output with lightweight expert vectors, reducing expert parameters while generalizing to any PEFT method. Notably, LiME introduces zero-parameter routing by leveraging existing frozen and adapted representations eliminating learned router parameters typically required per layer. Theoretically, we prove that (i) more experts preserve more task-relevant information and (ii) modulation approximates full expert-specific PEFT with bounded error. LiME further incorporates n-gram windowed routing and adaptive expert selection (Auto Top-K) based on routing confidence. Experiments on MMT-47, a multimodal multi-task benchmark with 47 tasks spanning text, image, and video, demonstrate that LiME achieves competitive or superior performance while using up to 4x fewer trainable parameters and up to 29% faster training compared to corresponding MoE-PEFT baselines.

</details>


### [60] [SIEVE: Sample-Efficient Parametric Learning from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02339)
*Parth Asawa,Alexandros G. Dimakis,Matei Zaharia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SIEVE：一种从自然语言上下文进行样本高效参数学习的方法，仅需3个查询示例，通过分解上下文生成合成数据并蒸馏到模型中


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言上下文（如指令、知识或反馈）包含丰富的信号用于适应语言模型。虽然上下文学习通过提示提供适应，但参数学习能持久化到模型权重中并进一步提升性能，但通常需要大量数据，严重依赖高质量轨迹或自动验证器。

Method: 提出SIEVE方法，使用新颖的合成数据生成管道SIEVE-GEN，基于"上下文可分解"的洞见。通过将合成查询与适用的上下文（而非全部上下文）配对，然后使用上下文蒸馏将上下文内化到模型中，生成更高质量的rollout。

Result: 在需要上下文的推理设置中（包括自定义领域、RuleArena和One Book机器翻译任务）进行评估。结果表明，SIEVE仅使用三个查询示例就优于先前的上下文蒸馏方法。

Conclusion: SIEVE展示了如何从自然语言上下文实现样本高效的参数学习，仅需极少查询示例即可获得显著性能提升。

Abstract: Natural language context-such as instructions, knowledge, or feedback-contains rich signal for adapting language models. While in-context learning provides adaptation via the prompt, parametric learning persists into model weights and can improve performance further, though is data hungry and heavily relies on either high-quality traces or automated verifiers. We propose SIEVE, a method for sample-efficient parametric learning from natural language context that requires as few as three query examples. SIEVE uses a novel synthetic data generation pipeline, SIEVE-GEN, that leverages the insight that context is decomposable. Decomposing context allows us to generate higher quality rollouts by pairing synthetic queries with only the applicable context rather than the entirety, then using context distillation to internalize context into the model. We evaluate in reasoning settings where context is necessary, including custom domains and the RuleArena and Machine Translation from One Book tasks. Our results show that SIEVE outperforms prior context distillation methods using just three query examples, demonstrating how to achieve sample-efficient parametric learning from natural language.

</details>


### [61] [Not All Denoising Steps Are Equal: Model Scheduling for Faster Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02340)
*Ivan Sedykh,Nikita Sorokin,Valentin Malykh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过模型调度策略，在扩散语言模型采样中用小型模型替代大型模型处理部分去噪步骤，显著降低计算成本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）采样成本高，需要大量全序列去噪步骤且无法利用KV缓存，而自回归模型可受益于KV缓存。需要降低MDLM采样计算成本的方法。

Method: 提出模型调度策略：在扩散过程中，用小型MDLM替代大型模型处理部分去噪步骤。通过损失和KL散度分析确定不同时间步的重要性，并进行粗粒度步骤段搜索，发现扩散轨迹中间部分最敏感。

Result: 在OpenWebText上，早期和晚期去噪步骤对模型替换更鲁棒，中间步骤最敏感。通过调度策略可实现高达17%的FLOPs减少，生成困惑度仅有适度下降。

Conclusion: 简单、架构无关的调度规则可显著加速MDLM采样，同时基本保持生成质量（通过生成困惑度衡量）。扩散轨迹中间部分对模型质量最敏感，应使用大型模型处理。

Abstract: Recent advances in masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) narrow the quality gap to autoregressive LMs, but their sampling remains expensive because generation requires many full-sequence denoising passes with a large Transformer and, unlike autoregressive decoding, cannot benefit from KV caching. In this work, we exploit the flexibility of the diffusion framework and study model scheduling, where a smaller MDLM replaces the full model at a subset of denoising steps. On OpenWebText, we show that early and late denoising steps are substantially more robust to such replacement than middle steps, enabling up to a 17% reduction in FLOPs with only modest degradation in generative perplexity. We support these findings with a step-importance analysis based on loss and KL divergence between small and large models across timesteps, as well as an exhaustive search over coarse step segments, both of which identify the middle of the diffusion trajectory as most sensitive. Our results suggest that simple, architecture-agnostic scheduling rules can significantly accelerate MDLM sampling while largely preserving generation quality as measured by generative perplexity.

</details>


### [62] [LLM Reasoning with Process Rewards for Outcome-Guided Steps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02341)
*Mohammad Rezaei,Jens Lehmann,Sahar Vahdati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PROGRS框架通过结果条件中心化处理过程奖励，将过程奖励视为结果组内的相对偏好而非绝对目标，有效利用过程奖励模型同时保持结果正确性的主导地位


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学推理强化学习方法主要优化结果正确性，但这对多步解决方案提供稀疏反馈，且对中间推理错误指导有限。过程奖励模型(PRMs)虽然能评分中间步骤，但其评分常与最终正确性不完全对齐，可能奖励局部流畅但最终错误的推理，导致奖励黑客攻击

Method: 提出PROGRS框架：1) 结果条件中心化：将错误轨迹的PRM分数在每个提示组内调整为均值为零，消除系统偏差同时保留信息排名；2) 结合冻结分位数回归PRM与多尺度一致性评估器；3) 将中心化过程奖励整合到GRPO中，无需辅助目标或额外可训练组件

Result: 在MATH-500、AMC、AIME、MinervaMath和OlympiadBench等数据集上，PROGRS持续改进Pass@1性能，优于仅基于结果的基线方法，且使用更少rollouts实现更强性能

Conclusion: 结果条件中心化能够安全有效地利用过程奖励进行数学推理，解决了过程奖励与最终正确性不对齐的问题，防止了奖励黑客攻击，同时保持了结果正确性的主导地位

Abstract: Mathematical reasoning in large language models has improved substantially with reinforcement learning using verifiable rewards, where final answers can be checked automatically and converted into reliable training signals. Most such pipelines optimize outcome correctness only, which yields sparse feedback for long, multi-step solutions and offers limited guidance on intermediate reasoning errors. Recent work therefore introduces process reward models (PRMs) to score intermediate steps and provide denser supervision. In practice, PRM scores are often imperfectly aligned with final correctness and can reward locally fluent reasoning that still ends in an incorrect answer. When optimized as absolute rewards, such signals can amplify fluent failure modes and induce reward hacking.
  We propose PROGRS, a framework that leverages PRMs while keeping outcome correctness dominant. PROGRS treats process rewards as relative preferences within outcome groups rather than absolute targets. We introduce outcome-conditioned centering, which shifts PRM scores of incorrect trajectories to have zero mean within each prompt group. It removes systematic bias while preserving informative rankings. PROGRS combines a frozen quantile-regression PRM with a multi-scale coherence evaluator. We integrate the resulting centered process bonus into Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) without auxiliary objectives or additional trainable components. Across MATH-500, AMC, AIME, MinervaMath, and OlympiadBench, PROGRS consistently improves Pass@1 over outcome-only baselines and achieves stronger performance with fewer rollouts. These results show that outcome-conditioned centering enables safe and effective use of process rewards for mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [63] [Haiku to Opus in Just 10 bits: LLMs Unlock Massive Compression Gains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02343)
*Roy Rinberg,Annabelle Michael Carrell,Simon Henniger,Nicholas Carlini,Keri Warr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究LLM生成文本的压缩，提出压缩-计算前沿概念，探索无损和有损压缩方法，并引入基于问答的交互式压缩协议


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何高效压缩LLM生成的文本，探索在压缩比和计算成本之间的权衡，寻求比传统传输完整响应更高效的知识传输方法

Method: 1. 无损压缩：使用领域适应的LoRA适配器改进基于LLM的算术编码；2. 有损压缩：先提示模型进行简洁重写，再应用算术编码；3. 问答压缩：小型模型通过向大型模型提问是/否问题迭代优化响应，每次回答传输1比特信息

Result: 1. 无损压缩：领域适应LoRA适配器比基础LLM压缩性能提升2倍；2. 有损压缩：压缩比约0.03，比原始响应压缩提升2倍；3. 问答压缩：在8个基准测试中，10个二进制问题可恢复小模型与大模型之间23%-72%的能力差距，压缩比达0.0006-0.004，比先前LLM压缩方法小100倍以上

Conclusion: 交互式压缩协议比传输完整响应能更高效地传输知识，问答压缩方法在极低压缩比下仍能显著提升小模型性能，为高效知识传输开辟了新途径

Abstract: We study the compression of LLM-generated text across lossless and lossy regimes, characterizing a compression-compute frontier where more compression is possible at the cost of more compute. For lossless compression, domain-adapted LoRA adapters can improve LLM-based arithmetic coding by 2x over compression with the base LLM alone. For lossy compression, prompting a model for a succinct rewrite then applying arithmetic coding can achieve compression ratios of approximately 0.03, a 2x improvement over compressing the original response.
  We further introduce Question-Asking compression (QA), an interactive lossy protocol inspired by the game 'Twenty Questions'. A small model iteratively refines its response by asking yes/no questions to a stronger model, transferring exactly one bit per answer. On 8 benchmarks spanning math, science, and code, 10 binary questions recover 23% to 72% of the capability gap between a small and large model on standard benchmarks and 7% to 38% on harder benchmarks, achieving compression ratios of 0.0006 to 0.004. This is over 100x smaller than prior LLM-based compression (Deletang et al., 2024), suggesting that interactive protocols can transfer knowledge far more efficiently than transmitting full responses.

</details>


### [64] [A Spectral Framework for Multi-Scale Nonlinear Dimensionality Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02535)
*Zeyang Huang,Angelos Chatzimparmpas,Thomas Höllt,Takanori Fujiwara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个基于谱分解的非线性降维框架，通过谱基与交叉熵优化实现多尺度表示，解决全局-局部保留权衡和可解释性问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决降维中的两个长期权衡：1) 全局与局部结构保留的冲突（如t-SNE/UMAP偏重局部但扭曲全局，Laplacian Eigenmaps保留全局但局部分离有限）；2) 表达能力与分析透明度之间的差距（许多非线性方法缺乏与高维结构的显式连接）

Method: 提出谱框架：结合谱基与交叉熵优化进行非线性降维，支持多尺度表示；利用线性谱分解从图频率角度分析嵌入，通过谱模式贡献理解嵌入过程；辅以基于字形增强的散点图可视化

Result: 定量评估和案例研究表明，该框架改善了流形连续性，同时通过谱模式贡献分析实现了对嵌入结构的深入理解

Conclusion: 提出的谱框架成功解决了降维中的全局-局部权衡和可解释性问题，实现了多尺度表示和基于谱模式的分析，为非线性降维提供了新的理论视角和实用工具

Abstract: Dimensionality reduction (DR) is characterized by two longstanding trade-offs. First, there is a global-local preservation tension: methods such as t-SNE and UMAP prioritize local neighborhood preservation, yet may distort global manifold structure, while methods such as Laplacian Eigenmaps preserve global geometry but often yield limited local separation. Second, there is a gap between expressiveness and analytical transparency: many nonlinear DR methods produce embeddings without an explicit connection to the underlying high-dimensional structure, limiting insight into the embedding process. In this paper, we introduce a spectral framework for nonlinear DR that addresses these challenges. Our approach embeds high-dimensional data using a spectral basis combined with cross-entropy optimization, enabling multi-scale representations that bridge global and local structure. Leveraging linear spectral decomposition, the framework further supports analysis of embeddings through a graph-frequency perspective, enabling examination of how spectral modes influence the resulting embedding. We complement this analysis with glyph-based scatterplot augmentations for visual exploration. Quantitative evaluations and case studies demonstrate that our framework improves manifold continuity while enabling deeper analysis of embedding structure through spectral mode contributions.

</details>


### [65] [DrugPlayGround: Benchmarking Large Language Models and Embeddings for Drug Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02346)
*Tianyu Liu,Sihan Jiang,Fan Zhang,Kunyang Sun,Teresa Head-Gordon,Hongyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DrugPlayGround是一个评估大语言模型在药物发现领域性能的框架，专注于测试LLM在药物特性描述、药物协同作用、药物-蛋白相互作用及生理反应预测等方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在药物发现研究中展现出巨大潜力，但目前缺乏对其性能的客观评估，无法确定其相对于传统药物发现平台的优势和局限性。需要建立一个系统化的评估框架来推动LLM在药物发现各阶段的应用。

Method: 开发了DrugPlayGround框架，该框架能够评估和基准测试LLM在生成药物相关文本描述方面的性能，包括：1) 药物物理化学特性描述；2) 药物协同作用分析；3) 药物-蛋白相互作用预测；4) 药物分子引入扰动后的生理反应描述。框架设计允许领域专家参与，为LLM预测提供详细解释，从而测试LLM的化学和生物学推理能力。

Result: 论文提出了DrugPlayGround框架，但摘要中未提供具体的评估结果数据。该框架旨在为LLM在药物发现领域的性能提供系统化的评估工具，促进LLM在药物发现前沿的广泛应用。

Conclusion: DrugPlayGround框架填补了当前缺乏LLM在药物发现领域客观评估工具的空白，通过系统化测试LLM的化学和生物学推理能力，有望推动大语言模型在药物发现各阶段的更广泛应用，加速药物研发流程。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are in the ascendancy for research in drug discovery, offering unprecedented opportunities to reshape drug research by accelerating hypothesis generation, optimizing candidate prioritization, and enabling more scalable and cost-effective drug discovery pipelines. However there is currently a lack of objective assessments of LLM performance to ascertain their advantages and limitations over traditional drug discovery platforms. To tackle this emergent problem, we have developed DrugPlayGround, a framework to evaluate and benchmark LLM performance for generating meaningful text-based descriptions of physiochemical drug characteristics, drug synergism, drug-protein interactions, and the physiological response to perturbations introduced by drug molecules. Moreover, DrugPlayGround is designed to work with domain experts to provide detailed explanations for justifying the predictions of LLMs, thereby testing LLMs for chemical and biological reasoning capabilities to push their greater use at the frontier of drug discovery at all of its stages.

</details>


### [66] [FTimeXer: Frequency-aware Time-series Transformer with Exogenous variables for Robust Carbon Footprint Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02347)
*Qingzhong Li,Yue Hu,Zhou Long,Qingchang Ma,Hui Ma,Jinhai Sa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FTimeXer：一种频率感知的时间序列Transformer，用于准确预测电网碳强度，通过FFT频率分支和门控时频融合捕捉多尺度周期性，结合随机外生掩码和一致性正则化处理不规则输入，在三个真实数据集上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电网碳强度预测对产品碳足迹核算和脱碳决策至关重要，但现有方法难以有效利用周期性振荡模式，且在面临不规则外生输入（如缺失数据、不对齐）时表现不佳。

Method: 提出FTimeXer：1）基于FFT的频率分支结合门控时频融合，有效捕捉多尺度周期性；2）采用随机外生掩码与一致性正则化的鲁棒训练方案，减少虚假相关性并增强稳定性；3）整体架构为频率感知的时间序列Transformer。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，FTimeXer相比强基线方法取得了一致的性能提升，能够提供更可靠的电网碳因子预测。

Conclusion: FTimeXer通过频率感知设计和鲁棒训练方案，有效解决了电网碳强度预测中的非平稳性、周期性利用不足以及不规则外生输入处理问题，为产品碳足迹核算和脱碳决策提供了更可靠的预测工具。

Abstract: Accurate and up-to-date forecasting of the power grid's carbon footprint is crucial for effective product carbon footprint (PCF) accounting and informed decarbonization decisions. However, the carbon intensity of the grid exhibits high non-stationarity, and existing methods often struggle to effectively leverage periodic and oscillatory patterns. Furthermore, these methods tend to perform poorly when confronted with irregular exogenous inputs, such as missing data or misalignment. To tackle these challenges, we propose FTimeXer, a frequency-aware time-series Transformer designed with a robust training scheme that accommodates exogenous factors. FTimeXer features an Fast Fourier Transform (FFT)-driven frequency branch combined with gated time-frequency fusion, allowing it to capture multi-scale periodicity effectively. It also employs stochastic exogenous masking in conjunction with consistency regularization, which helps reduce spurious correlations and enhance stability. Experiments conducted on three real-world datasets show consistent improvements over strong baselines. As a result, these enhancements lead to more reliable forecasts of grid carbon factors, which are essential for effective PCF accounting and informed decision-making regarding decarbonization.

</details>


### [67] [Contextual Intelligence The Next Leap for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02348)
*André Biedenkapp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出将上下文作为强化学习的一等建模原语，通过区分异源（环境强加）和自源（智能体驱动）因素的新分类法，推动实现真正的上下文智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管强化学习在游戏、机器人和连续控制领域取得了显著成果，但学习到的策略往往无法泛化到训练分布之外，限制了实际应用。现有方法将上下文视为单一静态可观测变量，这限制了RL智能体的泛化能力。

Method: 提出上下文的新分类法，区分异源（环境强加）和自源（智能体驱动）因素。识别三个关键研究方向：1) 异构上下文学习以利用分类层次；2) 多时间尺度建模以处理不同演化速度的上下文变量；3) 集成抽象高层上下文以纳入角色、资源制度等非物理描述符。

Result: 论文提出了一个概念框架而非具体实验结果。通过将上下文作为一等建模原语，旨在赋能智能体推理自身身份、世界约束及其随时间演化，从而催生新一代上下文感知智能体。

Conclusion: 通过将上下文提升为一等建模原语并采用系统分类法，可以推动实现真正的上下文智能，使智能体能够安全高效地部署到现实世界中，超越当前上下文RL的限制。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has produced spectacular results in games, robotics, and continuous control. Yet, despite these successes, learned policies often fail to generalize beyond their training distribution, limiting real-world impact. Recent work on contextual RL (cRL) shows that exposing agents to environment characteristics -- contexts -- can improve zero-shot transfer. So far, the community has treated context as a monolithic, static observable, an approach that constrains the generalization capabilities of RL agents.
  To achieve contextual intelligence we first propose a novel taxonomy of contexts that separates allogenic (environment-imposed) from autogenic (agent-driven) factors. We identify three fundamental research directions that must be addressed to promote truly contextual intelligence: (1) Learning with heterogeneous contexts to explicitly exploit the taxonomy levels so agents can reason about their influence on the world and vice versa; (2) Multi-time-scale modeling to recognize that allogenic variables evolve slowly or remain static, whereas autogenic variables may change within an episode, potentially requiring different learning mechanisms; (3) Integration of abstract, high-level contexts to incorporate roles, resource & regulatory regimes, uncertainties, and other non-physical descriptors that crucially influence behavior.
  We envision context as a first-class modeling primitive, empowering agents to reason about who they are, what the world permits, and how both evolve over time. By doing so, we aim to catalyze a new generation of context-aware agents that can be deployed safely and efficiently in the real world.

</details>


### [68] [OPRIDE: Offline Preference-based Reinforcement Learning via In-Dataset Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02349)
*Yiqin Yang,Hao Hu,Yihuan Mao,Jin Zhang,Chengjie Wu,Yuhua Jiang,Xu Yang,Runpeng Xie,Yi Fan,Bo Liu,Yang Gao,Bo Xu,Chongjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OPRIDE算法解决离线偏好强化学习中查询效率低的问题，通过信息最大化探索策略和折扣调度机制，显著减少所需人类偏好查询数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好强化学习(PbRL)可以避免复杂的奖励设计并更好地与人类意图对齐，但在实际应用中获取人类偏好反馈成本高昂且耗时，形成重大障碍。离线PbRL中查询效率低的主要原因是探索效率低下和学习奖励函数的过度优化。

Method: 提出OPRIDE算法，包含两个关键特征：1) 最大化查询信息量的原则性探索策略；2) 旨在减轻学习奖励函数过度优化的折扣调度机制。

Result: 实验评估表明OPRIDE显著优于先前方法，在明显更少的查询下实现强大性能。在各种运动、操作和导航任务中的实验结果验证了方法的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: OPRIDE通过高效探索和缓解过度优化，有效解决了离线偏好强化学习中的查询效率问题，为实际应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Preference-based reinforcement learning (PbRL) can help avoid sophisticated reward designs and align better with human intentions, showing great promise in various real-world applications. However, obtaining human feedback for preferences can be expensive and time-consuming, which forms a strong barrier for PbRL. In this work, we address the problem of low query efficiency in offline PbRL, pinpointing two primary reasons: inefficient exploration and overoptimization of learned reward functions. In response to these challenges, we propose a novel algorithm, \textbf{O}ffline \textbf{P}b\textbf{R}L via \textbf{I}n-\textbf{D}ataset \textbf{E}xploration (OPRIDE), designed to enhance the query efficiency of offline PbRL. OPRIDE consists of two key features: a principled exploration strategy that maximizes the informativeness of the queries and a discount scheduling mechanism aimed at mitigating overoptimization of the learned reward functions. Through empirical evaluations, we demonstrate that OPRIDE significantly outperforms prior methods, achieving strong performance with notably fewer queries. Moreover, we provide theoretical guarantees of the algorithm's efficiency. Experimental results across various locomotion, manipulation, and navigation tasks underscore the efficacy and versatility of our approach.

</details>


### [69] [Modeling and Controlling Deployment Reliability under Temporal Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02351)
*Naimur Rahman,Naazreen Tabassum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出部署中心框架，将可靠性视为由区分度和校准度组成的动态状态，通过多目标控制问题平衡可靠性稳定性与干预成本，在信用风险数据集上验证了选择性干预策略的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在非平稳环境中部署时面临时间分布漂移问题，现有缓解策略（如定期重训练和重新校准）通常关注孤立时间点的平均指标，未能显式建模可靠性在部署期间的演变过程。

Method: 提出部署中心框架，将可靠性视为由区分度和校准度组成的动态状态，通过序列评估窗口中的状态轨迹定义可测量的波动性，将部署适应问题形式化为平衡可靠性稳定性与累积干预成本的多目标控制问题，定义了一组状态依赖的干预策略。

Result: 在大型时间索引信用风险数据集（135万笔贷款，2007-2018年）上的实验表明，选择性、漂移触发的干预策略能够比连续滚动重训练获得更平滑的可靠性轨迹，同时显著降低运营成本。

Conclusion: 将时间漂移下的部署可靠性定位为可控的多目标系统，强调策略设计在塑造高风险表格应用中的稳定性-成本权衡中的关键作用。

Abstract: Machine learning models deployed in non-stationary environments are exposed to temporal distribution shift, which can erode predictive reliability over time. While common mitigation strategies such as periodic retraining and recalibration aim to preserve performance, they typically focus on average metrics evaluated at isolated time points and do not explicitly model how reliability evolves during deployment.
  We propose a deployment-centric framework that treats reliability as a dynamic state composed of discrimination and calibration. The trajectory of this state across sequential evaluation windows induces a measurable notion of volatility, allowing deployment adaptation to be formulated as a multi-objective control problem that balances reliability stability against cumulative intervention cost.
  Within this framework, we define a family of state-dependent intervention policies and empirically characterize the resulting cost-volatility Pareto frontier. Experiments on a large-scale, temporally indexed credit-risk dataset (1.35M loans, 2007-2018) show that selective, drift-triggered interventions can achieve smoother reliability trajectories than continuous rolling retraining while substantially reducing operational cost.
  These findings position deployment reliability under temporal shift as a controllable multi-objective system and highlight the role of policy design in shaping stability-cost trade-offs in high-stakes tabular applications.

</details>


### [70] [An Initial Exploration of Contrastive Prompt Tuning to Generate Energy-Efficient Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02352)
*Sophie Weidmann,Fernando Castor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出Contrastive Prompt Tuning（CPT）方法，结合对比学习和提示调优，旨在优化LLM生成能源高效代码的能力，以支持绿色软件开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的代码虽然功能正确，但相比人工编写的解决方案往往能源效率较低，这与绿色软件开发（GSD）减少代码能耗的目标相冲突。需要研究如何优化LLM以促进生成能源高效代码。

Method: 采用Contrastive Prompt Tuning（CPT）方法，结合对比学习技术（帮助模型区分高效和低效代码）和提示调优（一种参数高效微调方法，成本仅为传统微调的一小部分）。

Result: 在Python、Java和C++三种编程语言的问题上对三个不同模型进行评估。CPT在两个模型上实现了代码准确性的持续改进，但效率提升因模型、语言和任务复杂度而异，表明改进并非始终可靠。

Conclusion: CPT方法在优化LLM生成能源高效代码方面显示出潜力，但效果存在变异性，需要进一步研究以提高改进的可靠性和一致性。

Abstract: Although LLMs are capable of generating functionally correct code, they also tend to produce less energy-efficient code in comparison to human-written solutions. As these inefficiencies lead to higher computational overhead, they are in direct conflict with Green Software Development (GSD) efforts, which aim to reduce the energy consumption of code. To support these efforts, this study aims to investigate whether and how LLMs can be optimized to promote the generation of energy-efficient code. To this end, we employ Contrastive Prompt Tuning (CPT). CPT combines Contrastive Learning techniques, which help the model to distinguish between efficient and inefficient code, and Prompt Tuning, a Parameter-Efficient Fine Tuning (PEFT) approach that requires only a fraction of the cost of traditional fine tuning. This study evaluates CPT on Python, Java and C++ coding problems across three different models to provide a comprehensive evaluation. The method achieves consistent improvements in code accuracy for two models but efficiency gains vary by model, language and task complexity, indicating that improvements are not uniformly reliable.

</details>


### [71] [From Broad Exploration to Stable Synthesis: Entropy-Guided Optimization for Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02355)
*Han Song,Yucheng Zhou,Jianbing Shen,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EG-GRPO：基于熵分析的CoT+RL文本到图像生成优化方法，通过熵指导的策略优化实现更好的生成质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管结合思维链（CoT）和强化学习（RL）能改进文本到图像生成，但CoT的探索性与RL的优化性之间的相互作用机制尚不明确，需要系统性分析来理解这种交互并改进方法

Method: 提出基于熵的系统性分析框架，发现三个关键洞察后，设计了熵引导的组相对策略优化（EG-GRPO）微调策略，根据不确定性重新分配优化预算：低熵token被排除在奖励驱动更新外以保持稳定性，高熵token获得熵奖励以鼓励结构化探索

Result: 在标准文本到图像基准测试中，EG-GRPO实现了最先进的性能表现

Conclusion: 通过熵分析揭示了CoT和RL在文本到图像生成中的相互作用机制，并基于这些洞察提出的EG-GRPO方法有效提升了生成质量，为理解生成模型中的探索-优化平衡提供了新视角

Abstract: Combining Chain-of-Thought (CoT) with Reinforcement Learning (RL) improves text-to-image (T2I) generation, yet the underlying interaction between CoT's exploration and RL's optimization remains unclear.
